*Najiyya Younas,Omar Abdulkader,Yaser Ali Shah,Muhammad Jawad Ikram,Jebran Khan,Amaad Khalil*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种联邦学习（FL）框架，与经过结构优化的 YOLOv8 网络相结合，用于在 CT 图像上检测肾结石。系统允许多个医疗机构在不共享患者数据的前提下协同训练统一模型，兼顾检测性能与隐私合规（GDPR、HIPAA），并面向边缘端实时推理部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在医学影像诊断（如肾结石 CT 检测）中表现优异，但主流方案依赖中心化数据存储，容易引发患者隐私泄露和医疗数据安全风险，同时也难以满足 GDPR、HIPAA 等数据保护法规。医疗数据天然的分散性和敏感性使得跨机构集中数据既困难又高风险，因此需要一种既能利用多机构数据、又能保护隐私的协作训练范式。

Method: 作者将联邦学习与针对目标检测任务优化的 YOLOv8 结合：各医疗机构在本地数据上训练 YOLOv8，仅上传模型参数（而非原始患者数据）以聚合得到全局模型。架构层面引入残差特征融合（residual feature fusion）以增强特征表达与检测鲁棒性，并采用 DropBlock 正则化策略抑制过拟合。整体网络保持轻量设计，以支持临床场景下的边缘部署与实时推理。

Result: 实验在分布式 CT 数据集（分布于多个机构）上进行。评价指标为目标检测常用的 mAP@50（IoU=0.5 下的平均精度）。联邦 YOLOv8 模型取得 mAP@50 为 0.733，同时在训练过程中各机构的数据始终保留在本地，从而实现隐私保护。作者还指出该模型结构精简，可支持快速边缘部署与临床环境中的实时推理。摘要未提供与中心化基线或其它 FL 方案的详细对照数据、通信开销或收敛性分析。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献在于把联邦学习与改进型 YOLOv8 结合，用于肾结石 CT 检测，在保护患者隐私、满足合规要求的同时获得可用的检测精度（mAP@50=0.733），并具备边缘实时部署潜力，说明 FL 可作为当代医疗 AI 辅助诊断的安全可行方案。局限在于: 摘要仅给出单一 mAP 指标，缺少与非联邦基线的量化对比、客户端规模与数据异构性分析，以及隐私-精度-通信成本的权衡评估，方法性能的普适性仍需更系统的实验验证。

Abstract: Recent innovations in deep learning have significantly enhanced the diagnosis of medical images, although they are based on the use of centralized data storage that pose severe threats to patient privacy and medical data security. To address this issue, this research proposes a Federated Learning (FL) model that is coupled with an optimized YOLOv8 network to detect the kidney stones on a computed tomography (CT) image and at the same time, protect privacy of the patients. The suggested system can help various medical organizations to jointly train a common model without exchanging the information about the patients. This is to ensure that data protection laws like GDPR and HIPAA are adhered to. The residual feature fusion and DropBlock regularization among other architectural improvements are also included in YOLOv8 to enhance detection robustness and minimize overfitting. Experimental analysis carried out on a distributed CT dataset demonstrated that the federated YOLOv8 model has a mAP at 50 of 0.733 and is able to keep the data confidential. Moreover, its lean design facilitates fast edge deployment and real-time inference across a clinical setting. Altogether, these findings indicate that Federated Learning is a safe and efficient solution to AI-assisted diagnosis in contemporary healthcare when combined with the use of sophisticated object detection models.

</details>


### [29] [Region-Level Policy Optimization for Fine-grained MLLM Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19745)
*Yuheng Shi,Xiaohuan Pei,Minjing Dong,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Vision-RL2 提出一种面向多模态大模型（MLLM）细粒度视觉感知的"粗定位、精识别"两阶段框架：利用从 MLLM 注意力蒸馏出的轻量提议网络以粗粒度视角定位感兴趣区域（RoI），并通过区域级强化学习（Vision-RL2）以冻结 MLLM 阅读器的答案似然变化作为奖励来优化提议网络，从而在显著减少视觉 token 的同时提升细粒度识别精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM 的细粒度视觉感知通常依赖提高分辨率，但这会引入大量视觉 token，显著增加视觉编码与语言模型 prefilling 的开销。作者通过受控诊断发现：细粒度感知所依赖的两个操作——感兴趣区域定位（localization）与内容识别（recognition）——对分辨率的需求并不相同：定位可承受约 3–4 倍更强的 token 压缩，而识别则对分辨率更敏感。因此，合理策略是"从粗视图定位、将分辨率集中于被选中的证据区域"。然而现有方案存在缺陷：直接让 MLLM 解码坐标虽可端到端训练，但每次查询都需完整模型前向且依赖模型的 grounding 能力；从注意力蒸馏的轻量提议网络虽快，却继承了注意力目标的噪声，且 RoI 到答案之间是离散区域选择，答案保真度无法直接监督提议网络。

Method: 核心思路是"粗定位 + 精识别"的解耦，并用量化诊断（定位容忍约 3–4 倍更强压缩）支撑该设计。提出 Vision-RL2：将连贯区域（coherent regions）视为强化学习中的动作（actions），用一个冻结的 MLLM reader 对每个候选区域打分，分值为"移除该区域后答案似然的变化"，据此优化轻量提议网络。采用互补的减法（subtractive，抑制干扰性提议）与加法（additive，找回缺失证据）目标，仅更新预测器，无需区域标注、响应采样或推理轨迹。该区域级 RL 使离散区域选择对答案的保真度能够反向监督提议网络，突破前向不可导的限制。精炼后的提议进一步支持稀疏编码（sparse encoding）：放大证据区域、排除背景 token，从而在同等或更少 token 预算下保留高分辨率信息。

Result: 在六个细粒度基准（fine-grained benchmarks）和四种 MLLM backbone 上进行评估。主要结论：Vision-RL2 在每一个 token 预算下均较基座模型取得准确率提升；并且以约 4 倍更少的视觉 token 便超越了基座模型最大 token 预算下的准确率。实验覆盖不同 token 预算以验证效率-精度权衡，代码已开源（https://github.com/YuHengsss/VisionRL2）。

Conclusion: 主要贡献：(1) 通过诊断揭示定位与识别对分辨率需求的差异，为"粗定位-精识别"范式提供依据；(2) 提出 Vision-RL2，将区域级强化学习用于优化注意力蒸馏的提议网络，以答案似然变化为奖励，无需区域标注即可端到端提升提议质量；(3) 结合稀疏编码实现约 4 倍 token 缩减下超越全预算精度的效率-精度权衡。意义在于缓解 MLLM 高分辨率细粒度感知中的计算瓶颈。潜在局限：需要冻结 MLLM reader 提供奖励，训练时可能引入额外推理开销；奖励依赖答案似然，可能在答案模糊或多证据场景下的监督信号存在噪声。

Abstract: Fine-grained visual perception in MLLMs is commonly improved by raising the resolution, but the added visual tokens inflate vision-encoding and language-model prefilling costs. We show that the two operations underlying fine-grained perception, localizing the region of interest (RoI) and recognizing its content, have different resolution requirements. In a controlled diagnostic, localization tolerates roughly 3 to 4 times stronger token compression than recognition, which motivates localizing from a coarse view and concentrating resolution on the selected evidence. Decoding coordinates with the MLLM can be trained end-to-end from answers, but costs a full model pass per query and depends on grounding ability. A lightweight proposal network distilled from the model's attention is fast, but inherits the noise of its attention targets. The RoI from the proposal network reaches the answer through a discrete region choice, so its faithfulness to the answer cannot supervise the network. We therefore optimize the proposal network with region-level reinforcement learning, which we call Vision-RL2. It treats coherent regions as actions, and a frozen MLLM reader scores each one by how its removal changes the answer likelihood. Complementary subtractive and additive objectives suppress distracting proposals and recover missing evidence, updating only the predictor without region annotations, response sampling, or reasoning trajectories. The refined proposal further enables a sparse encoding that magnifies evidence and excludes background tokens. Across six fine-grained benchmarks and four MLLM backbones, Vision-RL2 improves accuracy over the base model at every token budget and surpasses its largest-budget accuracy with about 4 times fewer visual tokens. Code is available at https://github.com/YuHengsss/VisionRL2 .

</details>


### [30] [STAR: Structure-aware Test-time Adaptation for diffusion-based light field Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19747)
*Wontae Choi,Ki Ryum Moon,Jae Young Lee,Hyung Sup Yun,Il Yong Chun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: STAR 是首个用于从有限且含噪焦栈（FS）重建光场（LF）的测试时自适应（TTA）框架。它冻结预训练扩散先验，为每个测试光场拟合三个轻量级适配器，分别针对光场空间-角度结构的三个组成部分（视图内空间细节、跨视角角度依赖、跨视角视差）联合自适应，在双焦面与三焦面设置下均超越现有 SOTA，且推理时间更短。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从有限、含噪的焦栈测量中重建光场是一个高度病态的逆问题。尽管在给定光学装置下，光场到焦栈的成像几何是固定的，但光场的空间-角度结构（包括视图内空间细节、跨视角角度依赖、跨视角视差）因场景而异。因此，一个固定的预训练先验难以最优地捕捉每个测试光场各自的空间-角度结构。该问题对依赖光场进行重聚焦、深度估计等下游应用的成像系统具有重要意义，需要在测试阶段针对具体场景进行自适应以提高重建质量。

Method: STAR 采用扩散模型作为重建先验，并引入测试时自适应机制。核心做法：对每个测试光场，冻结预训练的扩散先验；设计三个轻量级适配器，分别对应光场空间-角度结构的三个组成部分——视图内空间细节、跨视角角度依赖以及跨视角视差；将这三个适配器拟合到观测到的焦栈上，联合自适应地调整先验表示。该设计使得自适应仅针对少量参数，避免了对整个先验网络的测试时参数更新。

Result: 论文在双焦面和三焦面两种设置下进行了实验，STAR 在两类设置下的重建效果均优于现有最先进方法。此外，相比于需要在测试时更新参数的方法，STAR 具有更短的推理时间。由于摘要未提供具体数据集名称、评价指标数值及对比方法的细节，相关定量结果需参见论文正文。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献是提出 STAR——首个面向基于扩散模型的光场重建的测试时自适应框架，通过冻结扩散先验并拟合三个针对空间-角度结构不同成分的轻量适配器，实现逐测试光场的先验自适应，在双/三焦面设置下取得优于现有方法的性能，同时保持较低推理开销。其潜在局限包括自适应仍需在测试时为每个光场进行优化（尽管参数少、推理更快），且有效性可能依赖预训练扩散先验质量与光场到焦栈成像几何的匹配性。

Abstract: Light field (LF) reconstruction from limited and noisy focal stack (FS) measurements is a highly ill-posed inverse problem. Although the LF-to-FS imaging geometry is fixed for a given optical setup, LF spatial-angular structure---including within-view spatial details, cross-view angular dependencies, and disparity across views---varies across scenes. Consequently, a fixed pre-trained prior may not optimally capture the spatial-angular structure of each test LF. We propose Structure-aware Test-time Adaptation for diffusion-based light field Reconstruction (STAR), the first test-time adaptation framework for reconstructing an LF from FS. For each test LF, STAR freezes a pre-trained diffusion prior and fits three lightweight adapters to the observed FS to jointly adapt the three components of the LF's spatial-angular structure. STAR outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods in both two- and three-focal-sheet settings, with shorter inference times than those with test-time parameter updates.

</details>


### [31] [Benchmarking MLLMs via Cognitive Expected Scene Graph for Safety-Critical Visual Negation Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19767)
*Zhiyun Jiang,Hanyong Wang,Binbin Liang,Yu Xie,Menglong Yang,Wei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出"安全认知下的场景否定理解"（SNUS）任务，将开放式视觉否定推理约束到安全这一具体认知维度，构建高保真否定式描述数据集，并提出基于结构的、极性敏感的评测指标CESG Score，揭示了现有MLLM与评估指标的肯定偏差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真正的机器智能不应局限于被动的像素配准，而需具备对"缺失信息"进行自上而下的功能性推理能力，即视觉否定理解。然而，无约束的视觉否定范式过于开放，且普遍存在的"肯定偏差"（affirmation bias）导致现有多模态大语言模型（MLLM）和评估指标在负向语义下均失效。作者认为需要通过锚定具体认知目标来界定否定推理的边界，而安全是一个高度实用且关键的认知维度，因此将问题收敛为安全认知下的场景否定理解。

Method: 1) 任务定义：提出SNUS任务，将视觉否定理解约束在安全认知的特定认知目标范围内。2) 数据构建：构建高保真否定式描述（negative caption）数据集，密集映射对局部危险（localized hazards）的断言，即通过描述"不存在的危险"来表达否定语义。3) 评测指标：提出认知预期场景图得分（Cognitive Expected Scene Graph, CESG）Score，该指标是结构落地（structure-grounded）且极性敏感（polarity-aware）的，用于在语义反转情形下正确评估模型输出。

Result: 作者开展了大量实验。结果表明：现有模型在SNUS任务上表现挣扎，难以处理否定语义；而传统评测指标在语义反转（semantic reversals）情形下完全崩溃，无法正确反映模型能力。相较之下，作者提出的框架为SNUS提供了扎实的基准，能够为风险感知的情境理解和反事实认知提供严谨基础。

Conclusion: 主要贡献包括：定义新的SNUS任务、构建高保真否定式描述数据集、提出极性感知且结构化的CESG评测指标，并系统性地暴露了MLLM的肯定偏差与传统指标的失效问题。其意义在于推动风险感知与反事实认知研究，为安全相关的视觉理解提供基准。可能的局限在于：抽象未给出具体数据集规模、模型架构细节与量化实验结果，且任务聚焦于安全这一单一认知维度，泛化到其他否定认知场景的能力有待验证。

Abstract: True machine intelligence requires transcending passive pixel registration to master top-down functional reasoning over absent information via visual negation understanding. However, unconstrained visual negation paradigms remain overly open-ended, and pervasive affirmation bias causes both existing Multi-Modal Large Language Models (MLLMs) and evaluation metrics to fail under negative semantics. To solve these intertwined challenges systematically, we first anchor the boundaries of negation reasoning within specific cognitive goals. Specifically, by focusing on safety as a highly pragmatic and critical cognitive dimension, we define the task of \textbf{S}cene \textbf{N}egation \textbf{U}nderstanding under \textbf{S}afety Cognition (\textbf{SNUS}). Under this framework, we construct a high-fidelity negative caption dataset mapping dense assertions of localized hazards. Concurrently, we propose the Cognitive Expected Scene Graph (CESG) Score, a structure-grounded, polarity-aware evaluation metric. Extensive experiments demonstrate that while current models struggle on the task, traditional metrics completely collapse under semantic reversals. Conversely, our framework delivers a solid benchmark for SNUS, providing a rigorous foundation to advance risk-aware situational comprehension and counterfactual cognition.

</details>


### [32] [AI Smart Glasses for Wearable Intelligence: From Egocentric Sensing to Agentic Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19793)
*Xu Yuan,Yi Wang,Zhuohang Jiang,Haohao Qu,Yujuan Ding,Shanru Lin,Guoliang Xing,Hongxia Yang,Jiannong Cao,Qing Li,Wenqi Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 这是一篇关于"AI智能眼镜（AI smart glasses）"的系统性综述，将智能眼镜定义为一种系统级概念，即由第一人称感知、资源感知计算、智能推理、多模态交互和真实场景约束共同设计的可穿戴智能平台。论文围绕硬件基础、可穿戴智能、交互设计、应用场景四个维度组织现有技术，并提出五大跨领域研究挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年AI的进步使智能眼镜从单纯的自我中心（egocentric）拍摄与显示设备，演变为在严格形态（form-factor）约束下实现实时辅助的可穿戴AI系统。然而，该领域技术分散在硬件、感知、交互、应用等不同方向，缺乏统一框架来组织技术、应用与开放问题。作者希望通过提出"AI智能眼镜"这一系统级概念，阐明自我中心感知与实时个性化辅助在物理世界中的协同设计需求，从而为该新兴领域提供统一的研究视角。

Method: 本文为综述性研究，方法是概念框架化而非提出新模型。作者将AI智能眼镜定义为系统级概念（egocentric sensing、resource-aware computing、intelligent reasoning、multimodal interaction、应用约束的协同设计），并围绕四个相互关联的维度展开组织：(1) 硬件基础，界定感知、计算、反馈传递与持续部署的边界；(2) 可穿戴智能，将自我中心信号转化为感知、情境（contextual）与智能体（agentic）能力；(3) 交互设计，涵盖用户在持续活动中请求、接收、纠正与调节辅助的方式；(4) 应用场景，横跨医疗健康、无障碍、情境学习、日常生活辅助、文化旅游与工业支持。在此基础上，作者提炼出五个跨领域研究挑战。

Result: 作为综述，本文不包含新的实验数据集或量化基准结果。其"结果"体现为组织性的分析产出：构建了一个可统一整理技术、应用与开放挑战的框架，并识别出五大未来研究方向——下一代硬件、可信自我中心智能（trustworthy egocentric intelligence）、终身个性化记忆（lifelong personalized memory）、主动式智能（proactive intelligence）与具身基础模型（embodied foundation models）。同时展示了不同领域需求如何反向重塑系统设计与评估方式。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于以"可穿戴智能平台"为核心视角，为AI智能眼镜提供了一个统一的综述框架，将硬件、可穿戴智能、交互设计与多场景应用串联起来，并明确提出五个跨领域挑战，为该方向的研究规划提供参考。作为综述，其局限在于未提供实验验证或定量评估，五大挑战与框架本身属于概念性与方向性论述，尚待后续具体研究加以检验与落地。

Abstract: Recent advances in artificial intelligence (AI) are reshaping smart glasses from egocentric capture and display devices into platforms for wearable intelligence. Smart glasses increasingly serve as wearable AI systems that connect first-person observation with real-time assistance under strict form-factor constraints. We frame this transition through the lens of \emph{AI smart glasses} and define them as a system-level concept in which egocentric sensing, resource-aware computing, intelligent reasoning, multimodal interaction, and real-world application constraints are co-designed for personalized assistance in the physical world. To systematically study this perspective, we organize the survey around four connected dimensions. First, we examine the hardware foundation that bounds sensing, computation, feedback delivery, and sustained deployment. Second, we study wearable intelligence, where egocentric signals are transformed into perceptual, contextual, and agentic capabilities. Third, we discuss interaction design, through which users request, receive, correct, and regulate assistance during ongoing activity. Fourth, we analyze application scenarios across healthcare, accessibility, situated learning, daily life assistance, cultural tourism, and industrial support, showing how domain requirements reshape system design and evaluation. We further identify five cross-cutting research challenges for future AI smart glasses: next-generation hardware, trustworthy egocentric intelligence, lifelong personalized memory, proactive intelligence, and embodied foundation models. By centering smart glasses as wearable-intelligence platforms, this survey provides a unified framework for organizing technologies, applications, and open challenges in this emerging area.

</details>


### [33] [Absence is Presence: Understanding Visual Scene Negative Events Under Safety Cognitive Constraint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19812)
*Zhiyun Jiang,Hanyong Wang,Binbin Liang,Yu Xie,Menglong Yang,Wei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出视觉场景"负面描述生成"（negative captioning）新任务，并给出基于反事实重建与对比解码（CRCD）的框架：将"物理缺失"转化为"语义负面事件"，通过合成安全期望场景与真实场景对比，识别图像中本应存在却因风险被忽略的关键缺失信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统场景理解只关注图像中客观"存在"的肯定性信息，忽略了"本应存在却缺失"的信息，而后者在安全攸关领域（如监控、自动驾驶、工业巡检）对风险预警至关重要。核心难点在于：需要把物理层面的"缺失"映射为语义层面的"负面事件"。现有视觉语言模型（VLM）难以胜任，原因有三：一是"肯定性偏置"（affirmation bias）压制负面推理；二是"心智补全"（mental filling）能力有限，难以推断完全缺失的物体；三是表示偏置使模型难以覆盖缺失类别。因此该任务具有重要研究价值，但此前缺乏系统性建模。

Method: CRCD 框架的核心思路受人类认知启发，将任务重构为"反事实潜在变化描述生成"（counterfactual latent change captioning），以绕过肯定性偏置。方法包含三部分：
1）双分支反事实重建架构：amodal completion 分支负责修复"残缺/遮挡的物体"，functional association 分支负责推断"完全缺失的安全物体"，从而增强模型的心智补全能力；
2）多条件表示学习机制：将通用特征投影到预定义的安全准则子空间，以缓解表示偏置，捕捉更多维度信息；
3）对比解码：通过解码"重建出的场景原型（安全期望）"与"原始输入（现实）"之间的特征级语义残差，约束不存在信息的搜索空间，从而激活解码器的负面逻辑。

Result: 摘要提到进行了大量实验（extensive experiments）验证 CRCD 的有效性，并为这一开创性任务建立了高性能基线。但摘要未给出具体数据集名称、评价指标（如 CIDEr/BLEU/SPICE 等描述生成指标）与量化结果，也未披露实现细节与消融设置，因此可报告的结果信息有限。

Conclusion: 主要贡献：提出"以安全为认知约束的视觉场景负面描述生成"新任务；揭示 VLM 在该任务上的肯定性偏置、心智补全不足与表示偏置三大障碍；提出 CRCD 框架（反事实重建 + 多条件表示学习 + 对比解码）并建立基线。意义在于将场景理解从"存在性描述"扩展到"缺失性风险评估"。潜在局限：摘要未提供量化结果与数据集细节，方法的可靠性高度依赖预定义安全准则与场景原型的质量，其泛化性与可扩展性尚待验证。

Abstract: Traditional scene understanding focuses on affirmative information objectively present in images. However, in safety-critical domains, comprehending key information that should exist but is actually absent is vital for risk mitigation. To bridge this gap, we focus on visual scene negative captioning with safety as the cognitive constraint. The core challenge is to convert physical absence into semantic negative events. Existing vision-language models (VLMs) struggle with this process because affirmation bias suppresses negative reasoning, while limited mental filling capability and representation bias further hinder the inference of absent information. To address these challenges, we propose a negative captioning framework based on counterfactual reconstruction and contrastive decoding (CRCD). Inspired by human cognition, CRCD reformulates the task as counterfactual latent change captioning to bypass affirmation bias. It contrasts a synthesized safe expectation with reality to identify semantic omissions. To address limited mental filling, we design a dual-branch counterfactual reconstruction architecture. The amodal completion branch restores defective objects, while the functional association branch infers completely absent safety objects. Concurrently, a multi-condition representation learning mechanism is integrated to mitigate representation bias by projecting universal features onto predefined safety criteria subspaces, thereby capturing information across more dimensions. By decoding feature-level semantic residuals between the reconstructed scene prototype and raw input, CRCD bounds the non-existence search space and activates the decoder's negative logic. Extensive experiments validate the effectiveness of CRCD, establishing a high-performance baseline for this pioneering task.

</details>


### [34] [SnapPhysics: A Physics-Aware Scene Graph from a Single View for Interactive Mixed Reality Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19815)
*Suji Kang,Seok-Young Kim,Young Bin Kim,Taewook Ha,Dieter Schmalstieg,Shohei Mori,Woontack Woo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SnapPhysics 是一个无需训练的框架，能从单张图像重建实例级三维物体，并估计质量、摩擦、重心等物理属性，通过将三维重建与物理感知场景图结合作为 VLM 的结构化上下文，实现物理一致的混合现实交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在混合现实（MR）中，物体的物理属性（质量、摩擦、重心）与几何形状同样重要，才能保证交互的物理一致性。现有方法存在两类局限：一是通过视频中物体动力学反推属性，计算成本高昂；二是直接对单张图像查询视觉语言模型（VLM），但缺乏几何接地（geometric grounding）和物体间关系建模。该问题对于无需人工调参即可部署的 MR 应用具有重要价值。

Method: 提出 SnapPhysics，一个 training-free 框架。核心做法是：结合实例级三维重建与空间对齐，构建一个物理感知场景图（physics-aware scene graph），该图编码物体间的相互关系以及每个物体的度量几何信息，作为结构化上下文输入给 VLM 进行物理属性推理。即把几何重建与关系建模作为先验结构化信息，替代纯单图 VLM 查询或视频动力学分析。

Result: 在 3D-FRONT 数据集上，场景级 F-Score 相比最佳学习型方法提升 18.6%。在含质量真值的真实采集场景中，平均绝对对数差误差（mALDE）相较仅用 VLM 的方法最多降低 20.5%，对数尺度相关性（r²_ls）最多提升 19.6%。

Conclusion: SnapPhysics 通过将实例级三维重建与物理感知场景图结合，为单图物理属性估计提供了无需训练且几何接地的解决方案，显著优于学习型重建方法与纯 VLM 估计方法，可支持无需手动参数调整的物理交互 MR 体验。局限在于主要面向刚性物体与常见室内物体属性，对复杂动力学或形变物体的泛化性尚未明确说明。

Abstract: We propose SnapPhysics, a training-free framework that reconstructs 3D objects and estimates their physical properties such as mass, friction, and center of gravity from a single image. For physically coherent interactions in mixed reality (MR), such properties are as important as geometry. Prior approaches infer them by analyzing object dynamics in video, which is computationally costly, or by querying vision-language models (VLMs) on single images, which lacks geometric grounding and inter-object relationships. We address these limitations by combining instance-level 3D reconstruction and spatial alignment with a physics-aware scene graph that encodes these relationships and per-object metric geometry as structured context for VLM-based property reasoning. Experiments on 3D-FRONT show that SnapPhysics improves scene-level F-Score by 18.6% over the best learning-based method, and on real captured scenes with ground-truth mass, it reduces the mean absolute log difference error (mALDE) by up to 20.5% and improves log-scale correlation ($r^2_{\mathrm{ls}}$) by up to 19.6% over VLM-only estimation. SnapPhysics enables physically interactive MR experiences without manual parameter tuning. Project page: https://snapphysics-ismar2026.github.io/.

</details>


### [35] [KoUniTalk: A Lightweight Articulation-Centered Korean-English 3D Talking Face Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19840)
*Hyunjung Chung,Unsang Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: KoUniTalk 是一个以口型发声（articulation）为中心的轻量级韩英双语 3D 说话人脸基准，通过形变迁移（deformation transfer）将 VOCASET 与韩国语音驱动的 3D 说话人脸数据统一到共享网格拓扑，实现跨语言、受控的语音驱动面部运动训练与评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高质量 3D 说话人脸数据集以英语为主，韩国 3D 面部运动数据因网格拓扑、空间尺度、坐标系和时间采样方式不同，难以与标准英语基准直接混用。这导致语音驱动的 3D 面部动画模型缺乏跨语言（韩-英）评测能力。作者希望通过一个统一表示空间解决数据异构问题，从而支持受控的跨数据集语言对比研究，而非提出新的形变迁移算法或完整头部身份保持的虚拟人数据集。

Method: 方法核心是基于 deformation transfer 的数据统一与重定向：将 VOCASET 与公开的韩国语音驱动 3D 说话人脸数据迁移到同一网格拓扑。构建的统一模板包含 1,176 个顶点，聚焦嘴部及相邻中下面部区域。该模板将输出维度从 15,069（VOCASET/FLAME）和 72,147（原始韩语网格）分别降至 3,528 维，对应 4.27 倍与 20.45 倍降维。为验证重定向是否保留语音相关运动，作者定义了语义嘴部关键点轨迹的量化指标，包括张嘴幅度、嘴宽、开口比（aperture ratio）和开口动态。由于韩国数据集官方测试集未公开，作者额外定义了说话人互不重叠（subject-disjoint）的韩语基准划分。需注意：文中指出数据集库存计数以来源报告值为准，与处理后计数分列。

Result: 实验与评测围绕重定向后的运动保真度与基准构建展开：通过语义嘴部关键点轨迹（张嘴、嘴宽、开口比、开口动态）评估重定向是否保留语音相关运动。处理后的匹配基准包含 22 位说话人、4,978 条序列、642,781 帧，支持在单一紧凑 articulation-template 空间进行韩英跨数据集评测。论文以评测设置与基准统计为主，摘要未给出具体数值精度或与基线模型的对比结果。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是提出 KoUniTalk——一个身份中性（identity-neutral）的规范输出空间与轻量级韩英双语 3D 说话人脸基准，通过统一拓扑与大幅降维，使语音驱动的面部口型建模可跨韩英进行受控训练与评测，并补充了 subject-disjoint 韩语划分。其局限在于：不提出新的形变迁移算法，模板仅覆盖口型相关的中下面部而非完整头部，不保留完整身份信息；韩语官方测试集缺失使基准划分由作者自定义；重定向引入的形变误差对下游动画质量的影响有待进一步验证。

Abstract: High-quality 3D talking face datasets remain largely English- centric, and Korean 3D facial motion data are difficult to combine with standard English benchmarks because of differences in mesh topology, spatial scale, coordinate system, and temporal sampling. We present KoUniTalk, a lightweight articulation-centered Korean-English 3D talk- ing face benchmark that retargets VOCASET and the released Korean speech-based 3D talking face data to a shared mesh topology using de- formation transfer. Rather than proposing a new deformation-transfer algorithm or a full-head identity-preserving avatar dataset, KoUniTalk provides an identity-neutral canonical output space for controlled speech- driven facial articulation training and evaluation across English and Ko- rean. The unified template contains 1,176 vertices and focuses on the mouth and adjacent lower- and mid-face regions, reducing the output dimensionality from 15,069 and 72,147 dimensions to 3,528 dimensions, corresponding to 4.27-fold and 20.45-fold reductions compared with VO- CASET/FLAME and the original Korean mesh, respectively. To exam- ine whether retargeting preserves speech-relevant motion, we evaluate semantic mouth-landmark trajectories, including mouth opening, mouth width, aperture ratio, and mouth-opening dynamics. Since the official test set of the Korean dataset is not publicly released, we additionally define a subject-disjoint Korean benchmark split. The processed matched benchmark contains 22 speakers, 4,978 sequences, and 642,781 frames, enabling Korean-English cross-dataset evaluation of speech-driven 3D fa- cial animation models in a single compact articulation-template space. Source-reported inventory counts are listed separately from these pro- cessed counts

</details>


### [36] [PACE: Precise AI Cinematic Expression: A Typed Specification for Script-Grounded Previsualization and Geometric Conformance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19853)
*Bing Duan,Qiang Guo,Linpu Li,Zhijian Mao,Min Zhu,Zhirui Ren,Yiwei Yan,Xi Chu,Xiaoding Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PACE（Precise AI Cinematic Expression）提出一种面向"剧本到影像"的分层类型化表示（typed representation），将空间规划问题显式建模：包括剧本证据、角色/道具/场景、每个主体站位以及摄影机行为。值在所属层级（script/scene/shot/panel）书写一次并向下层继承；编译器把该表示同时转译为扩散模型的提示词和以米为单位的 3D 场景，并由相机求解器使"声明的取景"与"构建出的取景"一致。核心主张是：对可几何化的声明值进行逐字段测量（而非让模型主观判断），并显式报告无法同时满足的残差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 剧本与成片之间存在一个"空间优先"的规划问题：谁站在哪里、摄影机从何处看到什么。直接用自由文本提示图像扩散模型生成镜头时，这一空间规划被模型的默认先验所替代，导致人物站位、摄影机取景与导演意图不一致，且这种偏差不可测量、不可追溯。作者认为需要把"计划"从生成过程中抽离出来，变为可声明、可编译、可度量的中间表示，从而在生成之前锁定空间语义。该问题对于分镜、虚拟预演（previs）、以及任何需要精确空间控制的生成式影视流程具有实际意义。

Method: - 表示：PACE 是一种分层类型化表示，字段按 script / scene / shot / panel 四个层级声明，值"在所属层级写一次，向下继承"，避免重复与冲突声明。
- 编译：一个编译器把 PACE 表示同时产出两条路径——(1) 发送给扩散模型的提示词；(2) 用米制单位构建的 3D 场景。
- 相机求解：相机求解器负责放置相机，使"声明的取景（declared framing）"等于"实际构建的取景（framing built）"，即把声明值落地为几何。
- 评估范式：不是让模型去评判结果，而是对每个可几何化的声明值逐字段测量编译后的相机与摆拍渲染相对声明的偏差；对多主体情形下单个相机位姿无法同时满足所有站位的情况，显式报告残差而不是将其吸收/隐藏。

Result: - 在 11 场景的 Automatic Drive 剧本上：所有单主体摆拍面板中，主体的实际位置与声明位置之差在帧宽的 1.2% 以内。
- 当画面包含 2 或 3 个主体时，单个相机位姿无法同时满足全部位置声明，作者选择报告该残差而非掩盖。
- 在 204 个外部导演-分镜镜头上的对比（以导演文字描述的"目标头高"为基准的比值）：仅用导演文字交付为 1.906；用编译后的提示词为 1.733；使用 greybox 控制为 0.955。作者指出"取景保持得最好的条件，反而最少地绘出所描述的动作"。
- 在 30 个镜头上显式声明姿态（pose）后，所绘动作从 58.9% 提升至 74.4%，同时取景未发生移动。
- 评测指标围绕两类：空间精度（相对帧宽的位置误差、头高比值/取景一致性）与动作还原度（所绘动作比例）。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于把分镜生成中的空间规划显式化为可继承、可编译、可度量的类型化表示，并配套相机求解器，使"声明即取景"，同时用逐字段几何测量取代模型主观评判，并诚实报告多主体不可满足的残差。其价值在于为生成式影视流程提供可控、可审计的空间接口，而非追求单一模型能力提升。局限由作者明确指出：转场（transitions）、拟合运动（fitted motion）以及对生成面板的人工审查仍为未解决问题；此外评估规模有限（11 场景剧本与 204 个外部镜头），多主体镜头中单相机位姿的冲突仅被报告而未被求解，取景精度与动作还原度之间存在此消彼长的权衡。

Abstract: Between a screenplay and a film sits a planning problem that is spatial first: who stands where, and what a camera sees from where it stands. An image diffusion model asked for a shot in free text settles that plan by its own defaults. We present PACE (Precise AI Cinematic Expression), a typed representation for the plan: the screenplay evidence, the characters, props and locations it needs, where each subject stands, and what the camera does. A value is written once at the level it belongs to (script, scene, shot or panel) and inherited below it. A compiler turns the result into both the prompt sent to the diffusion model and a 3D scene built in metres, and a camera solver places the camera so that the declared framing is the framing built. Where a declared value becomes geometry, PACE measures, field by field, how far the compiled camera and the staged render sit from the declaration, rather than asking a model to judge.
  On the 11-scene Automatic Drive screenplay, every staged single-subject panel places its subject within 1.2% of frame width of its declared position; with two or three subjects one camera pose cannot satisfy every position, and the residual is reported rather than absorbed. On 204 external director-storyboard shots, delivered head height is 1.906 times the staged target from the director's words, 1.733 from the compiled prompt, and 0.955 with the greybox control; the condition that holds framing best draws the described action least. Declaring the pose on 30 shots raises the action drawn from 58.9% to 74.4% without moving the framing. Transitions, fitted motion and human review of the generated panels remain open. Code: https://github.com/StudioPiLabs/pace-core

</details>


### [37] [Socialized UAV Cross-Task Learning: Towards Cross-Granularity Collaboration through Hierarchical Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19867)
*Xinjie Yao,Ruipu Zhao,Yunqi Zhu,Zhihe Fan,Zhoupeng Guo,Weihao Li,Zhen Wang,Qilong Wang,Pengfei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出跨粒度学习应被视为"层级交互调控"问题而非简单任务耦合，构建了面向UAV的检测-实例分割联合基准CrossUAV，并提出渐进式自适应框架CGSC，在层级间动态调控跨任务信息交互的时机、位置与强度，抑制负干扰并利用粗-细粒度互补性，在两任务上均取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务联合学习通常通过特征共享、知识蒸馏或辅助监督来实现"任务耦合"，但跨任务学习中的表征粒度与监督粒度不匹配，容易导致干扰（interference）、教师偏差（teacher bias）或单向坍缩（unidirectional collapse）。该问题在UAV感知中尤为突出：视觉偏移（visual shifts）与检测（粗粒度目标定位）—分割（细粒度像素级）目标天然构成粗/细粒度知识源。作者据此主张跨粒度学习本质是"层级交互调控"问题，并指出现有工作缺乏统一的评测平台来系统研究该问题。

Method: 提出CGSC（Cross-Granularity Socialized Collaboration）框架，是一个渐进式、自适应的跨粒度协作机制，核心在于调控跨任务信息交换的"何时（when）、何处（where）、如何（how）"：1）渐进式激活策略，随网络层级逐步开启跨任务交互，避免早期粗粒度信号对细粒度分支的过早干扰；2）自适应强度调节，依据各任务对交互的贡献度动态调整交互权重，以抑制有害干扰、保留粗-细粒度互补结构。同时构建CrossUAV基准，提供UAV场景下联合目标检测与实例分割的统一评测平台，用于系统研究跨粒度任务协作。

Result: 在CrossUAV基准上进行大量实验，采用目标检测与实例分割的常规评价指标（如mAP、mask AP等）进行评估。结果显示，CGSC在检测与分割两个任务上均取得一致的性能提升，验证了"层级动态交互"作为跨粒度协作有效机制的有效性，并表明自适应调控优于静态任务耦合方式。

Conclusion: 主要贡献为：1）将跨粒度学习重新定义为层级交互调控问题，并指出粗-细粒度监督不匹配引发的干扰、教师偏差与单向坍缩；2）提出CGSC渐进式自适应协作框架；3）构建CrossUAV统一基准，推动UAV跨粒度联合感知研究。意义在于为多任务/跨粒度感知提供了动态调控范式。潜在局限：摘要未给出具体数据集规模、基线对比数值与计算开销分析，框架在更广泛任务组合与其他领域（如事件相机、时序感知）的泛化性仍有待验证。

Abstract: Joint learning across heterogeneous tasks is often treated as task coupling through feature sharing, distillation, or auxiliary supervision. However, in cross-task learning, mismatched representational and supervisory granularities make such coupling prone to interference, teacher bias, or unidirectional collapse. We argue that cross-granularity learning is fundamentally a problem of hierarchical interaction regulation rather than simple task coupling. This issue is particularly evident in UAV perception, where visual shifts and detection--segmentation objectives naturally form coarse- and fine-grained knowledge sources. To systematically study this problem, we introduce CrossUAV, a UAV benchmark for joint object detection and instance segmentation that provides a unified evaluation platform for cross-granularity task collaboration. To address these challenges, we propose Cross-Granularity Socialized Collaboration (CGSC), a progressive and adaptive framework that regulates when, where, and how tasks exchange information across network hierarchies. CGSC progressively activates cross-task interactions and adaptively adjusts the strength according to task contribution, suppressing harmful interference while exploiting complementary coarse- and fine-grained structures. Extensive experiments demonstrate consistent improvements on both tasks, validating hierarchical dynamic interaction as an effective mechanism for cross-granularity collaboration.

</details>


### [38] [PART: Learning 3D Part Assembly and Retrieval with Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19872)
*Ruchao Bao,Wenzheng Wu,Chucheng Xiang,Zhongyuan Liu,Yuan Liu,Jinxin Dong,Ligang Liu,Ziqi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PART 是一个统一的、基于 Transformer 的框架，将"从零件库中检索零件并组装成目标形状"这一任务建模为集合预测（set prediction）问题，可同时完成零件检索与变长 6-DoF 位姿回归，并利用位姿估计与目标分割之间的对偶性进行联合训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D 组装在现代制造与数字内容创建中具有基础性地位。现有工作大多聚焦于"在预先给定的零件集合上"进行组装，即零件集合已知且固定；而更贴近实际的设定——给定目标形状与一个大规模零件库，需自动从中检索合适的零件并预测其位姿来重建目标——仍鲜有探索。该检索式设定面临三大挑战：(1) 组合爆炸的搜索空间随零件库规模指数增长；(2) 输出长度可变，不同目标所需零件数量不同；(3) 零件需连续 6-DoF 位姿估计。这些问题使得传统固定零件集合的方法难以直接迁移。

Method: 作者将检索与组装统一表述为集合预测问题，并设计了一个新颖的 Transformer 框架，能够以可变长度输出同时完成零件检索与位姿回归，从而避免对指数级搜索空间的显式枚举。其关键创新在于挖掘"零件位姿估计"与"目标分割"之间的对偶关系：通过联合训练以及一个新颖的分割增强优化模块（segmentation-enhanced optimization module），利用分割信息来辅助和精化位姿估计，反之亦然。该框架在统一编码器-解码器结构中端到端地处理检索与 6-DoF 位姿预测。

Result: 作者构建了一个包含 80K+ 形状的大规模数据集用于训练与评测。实验结果表明，PART 能够泛化到场景布局、图像目标和真实世界扫描等不同输入形式，验证了该方法在检索式 3D 组装设定下的有效性与迁移能力。摘要未给出具体数值指标，主要结论在于泛化性和对多种目标源（场景、图像、真实扫描）的适应性。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献在于：首次系统性地提出并形式化了从零件库中检索与组装的实用设定，将组合爆炸的离散检索与连续位姿回归统一为集合预测问题，并提出基于位姿-分割对偶性的联合训练与分割增强优化模块，同时贡献了 80K+ 规模的大数据集。其意义在于推动 3D 组装向更贴近真实应用的检索式范式发展。潜在局限包括：对大规模零件库的可扩展性、检索精度在库规模增大时的退化风险，以及摘要中未量化的计算开销与真实扫描场景下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: 3D assembly is fundamental to modern manufacturing and digital content creation. In this paper, we present PART, a unified transformer-based framework for 3D part retrieval and assembly: given a target shape and a part library, PART automatically selects the appropriate parts and predicts their 6-DoF poses to reconstruct the target. While prior work has achieved impressive progress on assembling a pre-defined set of parts, this more practical retrieval-based setting remains largely unexplored. The task faces three key challenges: (i) a combinatorially explosive search space that grows exponentially with library size; (ii) variable-length outputs, as different targets require different numbers of parts; and (iii) continuous 6-DoF pose estimation for part assembly. To address these, we formulate retrieval and assembly as a set prediction problem and design a novel transformer-based framework that retrieves parts and regresses their poses with variable-length output. Additionally, we exploit the duality between part pose estimation and target segmentation through joint training and a novel segmentation-enhanced optimization module. Finally, We curate a large-scale dataset of 80K+ shapes, and the results show that PART generalizes to scene layouts, image targets, and real-world scans. Project Page: https://iambrc.github.io/PART-project-page/.

</details>


### [39] [BINDER: A Latent Variable Model for Probabilistic Medical Image Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19875)
*Stefano Cerri,Amirhossein Hassankhani,Yaël Balbastre,Koen Van Leemput*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 BINDER，一个基于互信息配准准则的通用概率医学图像配准模型，通过引入隐式体素级对应关系作为潜变量，导出仅含闭式迭代更新的优化与 MCMC 采样算法，可在多模态高维 3D 形变配准中首次量化不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像配准需要在单模态与多模态场景下稳健工作，而多模态配准由于灰度关系未知、目标函数非凸，尤其困难。互信息（MI）是经典的多模态配准准则，但传统 MI 方法大多是确定性优化，缺乏概率建模能力，无法给出配准结果的不确定性估计；而在高维 3D 非线性形变配准中，不确定性量化对临床决策尤为重要，此前在多模态情形下基本无法实现。本文旨在用一个统一的概率框架同时解决稳健优化与不确定性量化问题。

Method: 核心是一个以互信息为配准准则的概率模型，中心设计是一种空间插值技术：假设待配准图像之间存在潜在的体素级对应关系（latent voxel-wise correspondences），即每个参考体素的强度由其对应浮动图像体素的强度通过插值生成，从而将对应关系显式建模为潜变量。利用这些潜变量，作者推导出专用的期望最大化式优化与 MCMC 采样方法，所有迭代更新均具有闭式解，避免了对复杂似然项的数值近似。将框架应用于非线性配准时，得到一个高效的 demons-like 优化算法；同时可构造对应的采样器，用于在非常高维的 3D 形变空间中进行后验采样与不确定性量化。作者以 BINDER（Bayesian INference for DEformable Registration）为名开源了代码。

Result: 论文在多种单模态与多模态配准任务上评估了所提的 demons-like 优化算法，报告其具有稳健的 out-of-the-box（开箱即用、无需逐任务调参）性能。此外，作者展示了一个对应的 MCMC 采样器，据其所述是首次能够在多模态配准场景下、针对非常高维的 3D 形变进行不确定性量化。代码与实现已公开于 GitHub（BINDER）。摘要未给出具体数据集名称、评价指标数值或与基线方法的定量对比细节。

Conclusion: 主要贡献有三：一是提出基于隐式体素对应关系的互信息概率配准模型；二是导出仅含闭式更新的优化与 MCMC 采样算法，并给出可在实践中替代传统 demons 的非线性配准求解器；三是首次实现多模态高维 3D 形变配准的不确定性量化。其意义在于把多模态配准从确定性点估计推进到贝叶斯后验推断，并对临床场景下的结果可信度评估有价值。局限性方面：摘要未提及在大规模或临床 4D/时间序列数据上的验证，MCMC 采样在高维形变下的计算成本与收敛性、以及潜变量插值假设在剧烈形变或不连续对应情形下的适用性，均有待进一步考察；此外，与实际临床工作流中常用工具的定量比较也未在摘要中给出。

Abstract: We propose a new probabilistic model for general-purpose medical image registration that builds upon the mutual information registration criterion. It centers around a spatial interpolation technique that assumes latent voxel-wise correspondences between the images being registered. By exploiting these latent variables, we derive dedicated optimization and MCMC sampling techniques that only involve closed-form iterative updates. When applied to nonlinear registration, an efficient demons-like optimization algorithm is obtained that shows robust out-of-the-box performance across a variety of monomodal and multimodal registration tasks. We also demonstrate a corresponding sampler that can quantify, for the first time, uncertainty in multimodal registration scenarios with very high-dimensional 3D deformations. Our code, which we call BINDER (Bayesian INference for DEformable Registration), is freely available at https://github.com/ste93ste/BINDER.

</details>


### [40] [SlugTrails: An Egocentric Benchmark for Floor Plan Localization in Large Buildings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19876)
*Yunqian Cheng,Roberto Manduchi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SlugTrails 是一个面向大型室内空间的平面图（floor-plan）视觉定位基准，基于 30 Hz Aria 眼镜的第一视角（egocentric）记录，覆盖三栋校园建筑、六层楼、约 22089 m² 平面图轮廓面积，并针对有限视场角下的三种几何采集方式（单帧行走、静止多视角扫描、带里程计的行走序列）提供统一协议。作者发现现有代表性几何/学习系统的官方权重在 SlugTrails 上近乎失效，但经微调后性能大幅提升，且微调权重还能跨数据集改善 LaMAR 上的泛化，说明当前平面图定位的瓶颈在于室内数据而非架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平面图（floor-plan）室内视觉定位可实现无需额外基础设施的定位，具有实际部署价值。然而现有方法大多在小型住宅环境中开发与评估，与真实部署中大型公共建筑（如校园、商场、办公楼）的场景差异巨大，导致方法的可迁移性和真实有效性缺乏可靠验证。作者指出，缺少大规模、真实感知条件（第一视角、有限视场角、多种几何采集方式）且带精确对齐真值的基准，是阻碍该方向进展的关键问题，因此提出 SlugTrails 来填补这一空白。

Method: SlugTrails 的构建包含三部分：1）数据采集，使用 Aria 眼镜以 30 Hz 记录三栋校园建筑、六层楼的第一视角数据，平面图轮廓总面积约 22089 m²；2）平面图处理，采用 CAD 派生的平面图，标注语义类别并生成 circulation space masks；3）轨迹对齐，利用激光测量（laser-surveyed）锚点将轨迹精确配准到平面图坐标系。协议设计覆盖有限视场角下三种实际几何采集方式：单帧行走（single walking frame）、静止多视角扫描（stationary multi-view sweep）、带里程计的行走序列（walking stream with odometry），从而在同一建筑与真值下比较面向不同感知条件的方法。评估选取五个代表性几何与学习系统，并在其原生感知配置下进行测试；作者还开展微调（fine-tuning from stock checkpoints）与从零训练（train-from-scratch）的对比实验，以分析数据与架构各自的贡献。

Result: 评估显示，官方发布的 stock checkpoints 在 SlugTrails 上表现接近零（行走单帧最高仅 0.004 R@1m30°），说明现有方法难以直接迁移至大型室内场景。在 SlugTrails 上微调后，所有可训练方法族在三种任务上均有提升，例如 F³Loc 单帧从 0.0 提升至 0.141，序列任务从 0.03 提升至 0.66，且随观测累积增益持续放大。微调权重在未使用 LaMAR 训练的情况下，还改善了跨数据集泛化：F³Loc 序列 R@1m 从 0.048 升至 0.143，UnLoc 从 0.063 升至 0.127。相比之下，仅用 SlugTrails 从零训练的效果远低于从官方权重微调，表明当前平面图定位主要受限于室内数据而非网络架构。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献是提出并公开了 SlugTrails——一个面向大型室内空间、基于真实第一视角感知的平面图定位基准，包含数据、语义平面图、精确对齐真值及覆盖三种几何采集方式的统一协议，并系统性揭示了现有方法在该场景下的严重泛化不足。其核心结论是室内数据而非模型架构是当前主要瓶颈，微调可同时带来域内性能与跨数据集泛化的提升。可能的局限性包括：数据仅来自单一机构的校园建筑、设备为 Aria 眼镜，模态与场景多样性有限；评估方法数量（五个）相对有限；微调对数据的依赖意味着结论可能受 SlugTrails 数据分布特征影响，其向其他大型公共建筑（如商场、机场）的推广仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Floor-plan-based indoor visual localization enables infrastructure-free positioning, but most methods are developed and evaluated in small residential environments unlike the large public buildings of real deployment. We introduce SlugTrails, a floor plan localization benchmark for large indoor spaces under realistic egocentric sensing: $30$ Hz Aria glasses recordings across three campus buildings and six floors ($22089$ m$^2$ of floor plan outline), CAD-derived floor plans with semantic classes and circulation space masks, and trajectories aligned into the floor plan frame using laser-surveyed anchors. One protocol covers three practical ways of gathering geometry under a limited field of view -- a single walking frame, a stationary multi-view sweep, and a walking stream with odometry -- so methods designed for different regimes are compared on the same buildings and ground truth. Evaluating five representative geometric and learned systems under their native sensing configurations, we find that stock checkpoints (official released weights) are near zero on SlugTrails (at most $0.004$ R@1m30$^{\circ}$ on walking single frames), while fine-tuning on SlugTrails improves every trainable family on all three tasks (e.g., F$^3$Loc $0.0 \rightarrow 0.141$ single-frame and $0.03 \rightarrow 0.66$ sequential), with gains compounding as observations accumulate. The same fine-tuned weights also improve cross-dataset generalization on LaMAR with no LaMAR training (sequential R@1m $0.048 \rightarrow 0.143$ for F$^3$Loc and $0.063 \rightarrow 0.127$ for UnLoc), whereas train-from-scratch on SlugTrails alone stays far below fine-tuning from stock weights -- evidence that floor plan localization is currently limited by indoor data rather than by architecture. We release the dataset, protocols, and tools at https://github.com/Head-inthe-Cloud/SlugTrails.

</details>


### [41] [BinoGen: Scaling egocentric binocular data for embodied visual perception and learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19881)
*Chunpeng Li,Ya-tang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BinoGen 是一个面向室内环境的自动化生成框架，可生成大规模、具身感知（embodiment-aware）的自我中心双目视觉体验，并附带多模态稠密监督信号，用于提升真实世界中的各类视觉感知任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身视觉感知依赖于智能体在环境中持续交互所积累的时序连贯视觉经验，但收集大规模自我中心双目观测及稠密标注成本极高。此外，视觉经验不仅受环境影响，还受观察者具身条件（视角高度、视场角、双目几何、运动方式等）的影响，这一因素在现有数据集中往往被忽略，缺乏可控研究手段。

Method: BinoGen 通过联合建模环境变化与观察者变化来自动生成数据：（1）生成式场景合成；（2）概率化物体实例化；（3）外观随机化；（4）随机轨迹生成；（5）可配置的双目相机设置。该框架输出同步的双目视频，并附带稠密多模态监督，包括深度图、光流、表面法线、语义图、物体坐标与相机位姿。

Result: 基于 BinoGen 构建了包含超过 2000 万张带标注图像的数据集。实验表明：将 BinoGen 数据纳入训练可一致地提升真实世界视觉感知，涵盖深度估计、目标检测与视频目标跟踪。此外，在同一环境中生成的人类启发式与小鼠启发式观测可用于受控研究具身条件对感知学习的影响：具身特化适配显著提升性能，而联合训练可使单一模型在两种具身上均具备竞争力。

Conclusion: BinoGen 展示了大规模、可控视觉体验对提升具身感知的有效性，其核心贡献在于自动化生成具身感知数据及多模态同步监督，并提供了研究具身条件影响感知学习的可控平台。可能的局限在于其聚焦室内环境，向室外或更复杂动态场景的泛化尚待验证。

Abstract: Embodied visual perception relies on temporally coherent visual experience accumulated through continuous engagement with the environment. However, collecting large-scale egocentric binocular observations together with dense annotations remains costly and difficult. Moreover, visual experience is shaped not only by the environment but also by the embodiment of the observer, including viewing height, field of view, binocular geometry, and motion through the scene. To address these challenges, we present BinoGen, an automated framework for generating large-scale, embodiment-aware egocentric binocular visual experiences in indoor environments. BinoGen jointly models environmental and observer variation through generative scene synthesis, probabilistic object instantiation, appearance randomization, stochastic trajectory generation, and configurable binocular camera setups. The framework produces synchronized binocular videos together with dense multimodal supervision, including depth maps, optical flow, surface normals, semantic maps, object coordinates, and camera poses. Using BinoGen, we construct a dataset comprising more than 20 million annotated images for supervised learning. We demonstrate two complementary utilities of BinoGen. First, incorporating BinoGen data consistently improves real-world visual perception, including depth estimation, object detection, and video object tracking. Second, paired human-inspired and mouse-inspired observations from the same environments enable controlled investigation of how observer embodiment affects perceptual learning. Embodiment-specific adaptation substantially improves performance, while joint training enables a single model to perform competitively across both embodiments. Together, these results demonstrate that large-scale, controllable visual experience can improve embodied perception...

</details>


### [42] [GS-PI: An Optimization-Decoupled Appearance Decomposition Approach for Generating PBR Gaussian Assets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19907)
*Jieting Xu,Rengan Xie,Zijian Huang,Zehui Jin,Rui Wang,Yuchi Huo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GS-PI 提出将三维高斯泼溅（GS）资产生成 PBR 材质的过程重构为"优化解耦"的几何条件扩散任务：从预训练高斯模型中提取点云，用条件扩散直接预测 PBR 属性，再通过可微光栅化蒸馏回高斯表示，得到可重光照的 PBR-GS 资产，无需代理网格与逐场景联合光照/BRDF 优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D Gaussian Splatting 在新视角合成上表现优异，但其外观是"烘焙"的辐射度，光照与几何/材质纠缠在一起，无法直接接入基于物理的渲染（PBR）管线进行重光照与材质编辑。已有逆渲染方法试图通过联合优化解耦材质，但材质、光照、几何多个目标的优化相互竞争，导致严重的解耦歧义和残留光照伪影（如高光被烘焙进本征颜色）。因此需要一个能规避多目标竞争、同时保证多视角一致性的解耦方案。

Method: 作者提出 GS-PI，将 PBR 材质生成表述为"几何条件化的 3D 点云扩散过程"：直接在 3D 域操作，天然保证多视角一致性，避免 2D 扩散方法中的像素对应困难。核心是多尺度跨视角条件机制，融合三类互补信息：(1) 全局语义先验；(2) 源视角锚定的光度线索（source-anchored photometric cues）；(3) 绝对空间学习的视角方向条件信号。该设计压缩复杂多视角证据，缓解跨视角投影错位，并防止镜面高光被烘焙进本征色。整体流程为三步解耦：从预训练高斯模型提取点云 → 条件扩散预测 PBR 属性（albedo、roughness、metallic 等）→ 通过可微光栅化将属性蒸馏回高斯表示，得到可重光照的 PBR-GS 资产。

Result: 摘要声明 GS-PI 优于近期逆渲染基线方法，且以"一次学习式扩散前向 + 短时目标驱动蒸馏"替代逐场景联合光照/BRDF 优化，并无需代理网格（proxy mesh）。具体的基准数据集、评价指标（如 PSNR/SSIM/LPIPS、重光照误差等）与定量数值在给定摘要中未列出。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献在于提出优化解耦的 PBR 材质生成范式：用几何条件化 3D 点云扩散替代联合逆渲染优化，从表示层面规避多目标竞争与 2D 扩散的跨视角对应问题，并以多尺度跨视角条件抑制高光烘焙，最终产出可重光照的 PBR-GS 资产且不依赖代理网格。其意义在于提升 GS 资产在 PBR 管线中的可编辑性、重光照能力与多视角一致性。潜在局限包括：性能仍依赖预训练高斯模型与所提取点云的质量，蒸馏阶段引入额外开销，且摘要未给出定量结果与对极端光照/高光场景的鲁棒性验证。

Abstract: Gaussian Splatting (GS) excels at novel-view synthesis but encodes baked-in radiance, tightly entangling illumination with geometry and preventing seamless integration into physically based rendering (PBR) pipelines. Existing inverse-rendering methods attempt to disentangle materials via joint optimization, but often suffer from competing objectives that cause severe ambiguities and residual lighting artifacts. To overcome this, we present GS-PI, a novel optimization-decoupled framework that casts PBR material generation as a geometry-conditioned diffusion process on 3D point clouds. By operating directly in the 3D domain, our method inherently guarantees multi-view consistency, sidestepping the severe pixel correspondence issues that challenge 2D diffusion approaches. We introduce a multi-scale cross-view conditioning mechanism that integrates three complementary components: a global semantic prior, source-anchored photometric cues, and an absolute spatial learned view-direction conditioning signal. This design efficiently compresses complex multi-view evidence, mitigating cross-view projection misalignment and successfully preventing specular highlights from baking into intrinsic colors. By extracting a point cloud from a pre-trained Gaussian model, predicting PBR attributes via conditional diffusion, and distilling them back through differentiable rasterisation, we yield a fully relightable PBR-GS asset. GS-PI outperforms recent inverse-rendering baselines while replacing per-scene joint illumination/BRDF optimization with a learned diffusion pass followed by a short target-driven distillation, without requiring proxy meshes.

</details>


### [43] [CitySTAR: Structured and Topology-Aware Reasoning for Open-Vocabulary Urban 3D Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19911)
*Shuai Zhang,Hongye Hou,Qinghe Liu,Zhuoxiao Li,Dongli Wu,Jing Ou,Yuan Liu,Wufan Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CitySTAR 将城市级 3D 定位重新定义为"结构化约束推理"问题：把自然语言描述解析为关于开放词汇 3D 实体、属性与空间关系的可计算跨模态约束，通过免训练（training-free）框架在十亿级城市点云上完成推理式 3D grounding，并配套提出语义与几何标注更完善的 CitySTAR-3D 基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D grounding 是具身感知与空间推理的基础任务，需要根据自然语言在复杂场景中定位目标实体。但现有方法大多依赖特征相似度或直接匹配，难以将自然语言的语义意图与十亿级城市点云中隐含的语义结构和几何结构关联起来。城市尺度的点云规模巨大、目标开放词汇、空间关系复杂，特征匹配范式在可解释性和泛化性上都受限。因此作者主张从"匹配"转向"结构化约束推理"，以显式、可计算的约束来桥接语言与 3D 场景。

Method: 核心是把描述语义组织为跨模态可计算约束。(1) 将原始十亿级城市点云提升为"查询就绪"的开放词汇 3D 实例场景图（scene graph），节点为开放词汇 3D 实例。(2) 由 CodeLLM 驱动的工具链为节点属性与 3D 空间关系提供多模态证据。(3) 用成对超图（paired hypergraphs）建模目标—上下文拓扑，并做双向拓扑验证以实现结构消歧。(4) 提出 Reflective Cross-modal Grounding 模块，融合拓扑一致性与以候选为中心的 2D 视觉证据，在度量感知的 3D 上下文图上做决策。整体为 training-free，无需针对该任务训练网络。

Result: 作者构建了 CitySTAR-3D 基准，相较已有城市级 3D grounding 基准在语义覆盖度、实例完整性、包围盒保真度和空间关系复杂度四个维度上有所增强。实验表明 CitySTAR 在开放世界城市 3D grounding 上取得一致提升，同时保持较强的可解释性与泛化能力。摘要未给出具体数据集规模、指标数值或消融细节。

Conclusion: 主要贡献包括：将城市级 3D grounding 重定义为结构化约束推理、提出免训练的推理驱动框架 CitySTAR（场景图 + CodeLLM 工具 + 超图拓扑验证 + 反射式跨模态 grounding），以及发布更完善的城市尺度基准 CitySTAR-3D。方法强调可解释性与开放词汇泛化。潜在局限：依赖开放词汇 3D 实例分割与 CodeLLM 工具链的质量，引入 2D 视觉证据需要图像与点云配准；免训练流程在十亿级点云上的推理效率与可扩展性，以及超图与多模块带来的人工设计成本，摘要中未作说明。

Abstract: 3D grounding aims to localize target entities in complex scenes from natural language and plays a fundamental role in embodied perception and spatial reasoning. However, existing approaches mostly rely on feature similarity or direct matching, making it difficult to connect natural-language intent with the implicit semantic and geometric structures hidden in billion-scale urban point clouds. We reformulate city-scale 3D grounding as structured constraint reasoning, where description semantics are organized into computable cross-modal constraints over open-vocabulary 3D entities, attributes, and spatial relations. We present CitySTAR, a training-free framework for reasoning-driven urban 3D grounding. CitySTAR lifts raw billion-scale urban point clouds into a query-ready scene graph of open-vocabulary 3D instances, with CodeLLM-driven tools supplying multimodal evidence for node attributes and 3D spatial relations. It then models target-context topology with paired hypergraphs and performs bidirectional topology verification for structural disambiguation. Finally, a Reflective Cross-modal Grounding module integrates topology consistency and candidate-centered 2D visual evidence to make decisions over a metric-aware 3D context graph. To further support this setting, we introduce CitySTAR-3D, an enhanced benchmark that improves semantic coverage, instance completeness, bounding-box fidelity, and spatial-relation complexity in city-scale 3D grounding. Extensive experiments show that CitySTAR consistently improves open-world urban 3D grounding while maintaining strong interpretability and generalization.

</details>


### [44] [DirtyMoCap: Robust Motion Capture from Unconstrained Markers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19927)
*Long Wang,Shuting Zhao,Shen Yan,Siyuan Yu,Xiaoben Li,Zeyu Cai,Yumeng Hou,Yuliang Xiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DirtyMoCap：一种与标记布局无关的鲁棒人体运动捕捉框架，将无约束（稀疏、带噪、无序）标记点云映射到由骨骼关节与体表点构成的"代理锚点"中间表示，再通过可微高斯-牛顿求解器拟合SMPL-H模型，实现单模型对任意标记配置的泛化并大幅加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统光学动作捕捉依赖严格的标记点布局与干净轨迹，才能获得高保真人体运动。然而在真实场景中，追踪系统输出的往往是稀疏、含噪、无序且配置未知或变化的标记点云，这使既有方法难以直接应用。已有参数化人体模型拟合方法多针对特定标记配置训练，缺乏对任意布局的泛化能力。论文旨在弥合"受损原始标记"与"参数化人体模型（如SMPL-H）"之间的差距，提升动作捕捉在非受控条件下的实用性。

Method: 核心思想是引入固定数量的"代理锚点（proxy anchors）"，由骨骼关节与人体表面点组成，作为无序标记与参数化模型之间的稳定中间表示。流程分两步：(1) 采用循环滑动窗口（recurrent sliding-window）架构在长序列上初始化并跟踪这些锚点，保证时序一致性；(2) 使用定制的可微高斯-牛顿求解器将 SMPL-H 模型拟合到跟踪到的锚点，恢复全身姿态、平移与体型。关键创新在于显式推导几何残差，使观测置信度、平滑项与先验项的权重能够端到端学习，从而依据输入可靠性动态自适应调整。此外实现了定制 CUDA 求解器以提升计算效率。

Result: 在多种带噪、不同标记配置的数据上开展实验。结果显示，DirtyMoCap 仅用单一训练模型即可泛化到任意标记布局，并在关节与顶点重建精度上持续优于针对特定配置训练的最先进基线。定制 CUDA 求解器相较标准 PyTorch 实现最高取得约 100 倍加速。作者进一步将方法应用于异构的原始光学动作捕捉记录——传统中国武术（Kung Fu），构建了时序连贯的 SMPL-H 重建数据集。

Conclusion: 贡献有三：提出标记布局无关的鲁棒 MoCap 框架；设计代理锚点中间表示配合可微高斯-牛顿拟合，端到端学习自适应权重；提供高效 CUDA 求解器与 Kung Fu 运动数据集。方法在非受控、脏标记条件下具备实用价值与泛化性。潜在局限包括：依赖 SMPL-H 参数化人体模型的先验表达能力、对极端遮挡或超大噪声的鲁棒性未充分说明，且评测主要集中于无约束标记场景，尚未涉及纯视觉或事件相机等无标记输入，实时性能亦需进一步验证。

Abstract: Optical motion capture delivers high-fidelity human motion, but its reliance on strict marker layouts and clean trajectories severely limits its real-world applicability. In practice, tracking systems frequently output unconstrained markers: sparse, noisy, and unordered point clouds with unknown or varying configurations. To bridge the gap between corrupted raw markers and parametric human models, we introduce DirtyMoCap, a robust, marker-layout-free framework. Our core insight is to map unordered marker observations to a fixed set of "proxy anchors" comprising skeletal joints and body surface points, which serve as a stable intermediate representation. We first initialize and track these anchors over long sequences using a recurrent sliding-window architecture. Then, a custom differentiable Gauss-Newton solver fits the SMPL-H model to the tracked anchors to recover full-body pose, translation, and shape. By explicitly deriving geometric residuals, our solver learns adaptive observation confidence, smoothness, and prior weights end-to-end, adapting dynamically to the reliability of the input data. Extensive experiments on diverse, noisy marker configurations demonstrate that DirtyMoCap successfully generalizes across arbitrary layouts using only a single trained model. It consistently outperforms state-of-the-art configuration-specific baselines in both joint and vertex reconstruction accuracy, while our custom CUDA solver achieves up to a 100x speedup over standard PyTorch implementations. We further apply DirtyMoCap to heterogeneous raw optical MoCap recordings of traditional Chinese martial arts, yielding a Kung Fu motion dataset of temporally coherent SMPL-H reconstructions. Code and data are available at https://wanglongzju.github.io/DirtyMoCap-Project-Page.

</details>


### [45] [Enhanced Knowledge Distillation for Detection Transformer via Teacher Prediction Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19964)
*Yitong Xing,Yuhao Cheng,Yanping Li,Yichao Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出教师预测精炼蒸馏（TPRD），一个即插即用模块，在校师生蒸馏前利用DETR各阶段的预测信息对教师预测进行精炼，通过正预测校正（PPC）、负预测抑制（NPS）和最大暗知识保留（MDKP）提升监督质量，从而改善边缘设备上紧凑DETR模型的检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DETR类检测器性能强但计算开销大，难以部署在边缘设备上，因此需要知识蒸馏来压缩模型。然而现有DETR蒸馏方法主要关注对齐蒸馏点（如特征、query、logits）的设计，却忽视了教师监督信号本身的质量。作者观察到DETR存在阶段间非单调的预测行为：早期阶段定位准确或分类正确的预测在后续阶段可能退化，而某些负样本预测会变得过度自信。这使得仅依赖当前阶段预测会产生不准确且不一致的监督信号，进而误导学生模型。因此，提升教师监督质量是提升蒸馏效果的关键问题。

Method: TPRD是一个即插即用的教师预测精炼模块，核心在于蒸馏前利用DETR解码器各阶段的预测信息重新构造更可靠的监督信号，包含三个组件：(1) 正预测校正（PPC）：针对在后续阶段发生退化的正样本预测，从更早阶段恢复更准确的定位与分类预测，保证可靠的定位和分类监督；(2) 负预测抑制（NPS）：抑制过度自信的负样本预测，避免其向学生传递误导性监督；(3) 最大暗知识保留（MDKP）：为避免精炼过程破坏暗知识（dark knowledge），选择性地只精炼目标类别的logits，同时保留非目标类别之间的关系信息。整体方法不改变原有蒸馏框架结构，可与其他DETR蒸馏方法结合使用。

Result: 在MS COCO和PASCAL VOC数据集上进行了大量实验，验证了方法的有效性与鲁棒性。论文声称TPRD作为即插即用模块能够稳定提升蒸馏性能，但摘要中未给出具体的AP数值、学生模型骨干、教师模型配置及与基线方法的定量对比细节。代码已开源于 https://github.com/xingyitong1/TPRD 。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于首次明确指出DETR蒸馏中教师监督质量被忽视的问题，并观察到阶段间非单调预测退化与负样本过度自信现象，提出TPRD以PPC、NPS和MDKP三个组件精炼教师预测，同时保留暗知识。方法具有即插即用、通用性强的特点，有利于紧凑DETR在边缘设备上的部署。摘要未披露具体量化结果、计算开销分析及对不同DETR变体的泛化性验证，精炼过程对推理阶段是否引入额外成本也尚不明确。

Abstract: Detection Transformers (DETRs) achieve strong performance in object detection but remain challenging to deploy on edge devices due to their high computational cost. Existing DETR distillation methods mainly focus on aligning distillation points, while largely overlooking the quality of the teacher's supervision itself. We observe that due to stage-wise non-monotonic prediction behavior in DETRs, well-localized or correctly classified predictions from earlier stages may degrade in later ones, and some negative predictions become increasingly overconfident. As a result, relying solely on the current stage's predictions yields inaccurate and inconsistent supervision. To address this issue, we propose Teacher Prediction Refinement Distillation (TPRD), a plug-and-play module that refines teacher predictions before distillation by exploiting stage-wise prediction information. TPRD improves supervision quality through Positive Prediction Correction (PPC), which corrects degraded positive predictions by restoring more accurate ones from earlier stages, ensuring reliable localization and classification signals, and Negative Prediction Suppression (NPS) suppresses the influence of overconfident negatives, preventing them from providing misleading supervision to the student. To preserve informative dark knowledge, we further introduce Maximum Dark Knowledge Preservation (MDKP), which selectively refines target-class logits while retaining non-target relations. Extensive experiments on MS COCO and PASCAL VOC demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of the proposed method. Our code is available at https://github.com/xingyitong1/TPRD.

</details>


### [46] [Beyond the Foreground: FOV-Aware Polyp Image Synthesis via Lesion-Guided Adaptive Mucosal Context Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19966)
*Tong Wang,Yuting He,Bin Ren,Yutong Xie,Guanyu Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LAMP 是首个面向结肠镜息肉图像合成的"前景引导"框架，通过"病灶引导的自适应黏膜上下文传播"来生成与给定病灶兼容的黏膜背景，并在五个息肉数据集上显著超越现有方法且稳定提升下游分割模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结肠镜标注稀缺，合成"图像-掩码"对可缓解标注压力，但医学图像合成要求既保留给定病灶外观、又生成解剖与光学上兼容的周围黏膜。现有前景引导方法把所有非前景像素一概视为背景，且主要依赖局部整合（local integration）。直接迁移到结肠镜场景会带来两个问题：(1) 视野外的黑色非黏膜区域（camera exterior）会污染生成的黏膜组织；(2) 局部推理导致黏膜纹理与光照不一致。因此需要一个能够显式区分病灶、有效黏膜与镜外区域，并进行全局上下文传播的合成框架。

Method: LAMP 的核心是"病灶引导的自适应黏膜上下文传播"：1) 用视场（FOV）掩码显式地将图像划分为病灶（lesion）、有效黏膜（valid mucosa）与镜头外区域（camera exterior）三类，避免黑色非黏膜像素被当作背景参与生成；2) 设计病灶-黏膜交叉注意力（Lesion-to-Mucosa cross-attention），将病灶外观编码为条件，注入到有效黏膜位置，实现外观一致的条件化；3) 提出受 FOV 约束的多方向 Vision Receptance Weighted Key Value（Vision RWKV）模块，在合法的组织支撑区域（legal tissue support）上进行多方向上下文传播，克服局部整合造成的纹理与光照不连续；4) 通过自适应门控（adaptive gate）将上述传播特征以残差方式融合进扩散 U-Net，从而在不破坏扩散先验的前提下调制生成过程。

Result: 在五个息肉数据集上进行实验，从整体生成质量（overall generation quality）与下游任务两方面评估：LAMP 在生成质量上大幅优于现有方法，并且作为数据增强手段，能一致地提升五种下游分割模型的性能。论文未在摘要中给出具体指标数值，代码将开源于 https://github.com/wangtong627/LAMP。

Conclusion: 贡献：提出首个前景引导的息肉合成框架 LAMP，通过 FOV 掩码显式区分病灶/有效黏膜/镜外区域，结合病灶-黏膜交叉注意力与 FOV 约束的多方向 Vision RWKV 做上下文传播，并用自适应门控残差注入扩散 U-Net，同时改善生成质量与下游分割。意义：为标注稀缺的结肠镜场景提供了更解剖与光学一致的合成数据方案。局限：摘要未给出量化指标与失败案例分析，方法依赖准确的 FOV 掩码与病灶-掩码配对，FOV 分割误差可能影响传播范围；同时评估主要围绕息肉（polyp）场景，对其他内镜病灶的泛化性尚不明确。

Abstract: Synthetic image and mask pairs can alleviate scarce colonoscopy annotations, but realistic synthesis requires preserving the supplied lesion while generating compatible mucosa. Existing foreground-guided methods treat all non-foreground pixels as background and rely mainly on local integration. Directly applying them to colonoscopy causes two problems: non-mucosal black regions contaminate generated tissue, and local reasoning produces inconsistent mucosal texture and illumination. We propose LAMP, the first foreground-guided framework for polyp image synthesis based on lesion-guided adaptive mucosal context propagation. LAMP explicitly separates the lesion, valid mucosa, and camera exterior using a field-of-view (FOV) mask. Lesion-to-Mucosa cross-attention extracts lesion appearance conditions for valid-mucosa locations, while FOV-constrained multidirectional Vision Receptance Weighted Key Value propagates them over legal tissue support. An adaptive gate then controls their residual fusion into the diffusion U-Net. Extensive experiments on five polyp datasets demonstrate that LAMP substantially outperforms existing methods in overall generation quality and consistently improves five downstream segmentation models. Our code will be released at https://github.com/wangtong627/LAMP.

</details>


### [47] [An Event Preserving Velocity Invariant Representation for Event Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19973)
*Mikihiro Ikura,Luna Gava,Jiahang Wu,Chiara Bartolozzi,Arren Glover*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SCARF（Set of Centre Active Receptive Fields）是一种面向事件相机的实时、速度不变（velocity-invariant）事件流表示方法。它保留原始事件而非转换为类图像帧，在快速运动、静止场景和独立运动物体等条件下均能稳定工作，并在计算效率和表示质量上均达到当前最优水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机通过异步、独立的像素电路实现了低延迟、高时间分辨率的感知能力，适用于机器人等实时视觉任务。然而这种新型电路也带来了算法层面的新挑战：在慢速运动下容易出现观测缺失，在快速运动下容易出现运动模糊。现有的速度不变表示方法虽然缓解了这些问题，但大多将事件转换为类图像（image-like）表示，从而丢弃了时间信息，损失了事件相机最核心的时间维特性。

Method: 论文提出 SCARF，一种基于"中心激活感受野集合"的表示方式。核心思路是构建一组中心激活的感受野，以集合（set）形式组织事件，而非将其压平成图像帧。该表示在设计上具备速度不变性：既能保留原始事件的稀疏与时间特性，又能一致地应对三类场景——快速运动、静止场景以及独立运动物体（independently moving objects, IMO）。由于避免了显式的稠密图像化转换，SCARF 在计算上保持高效，适合实时应用。

Result: 摘要声明 SCARF 在计算效率与表示质量两方面均取得了 state-of-the-art 表现。具体数据集、评价指标与定量结果在给定的摘要中未列出，需要参考正文以获取实验细节（如所用的机器人感知任务基准、延迟/吞吐量指标及质量评估协议）。

Conclusion: SCARF 的主要贡献在于提出了一种保留原始事件、同时具备速度不变性的实时事件流表示，能够统一处理快速运动、静止场景与独立运动物体，并在效率和质量上超越现有方法。其意义在于将事件相机的时间信息优势与实时机器人感知需求相结合。潜在局限（基于摘要推断）包括：具体下游任务泛化性、真实硬件部署验证以及定量基准的全面性仍需正文进一步说明。

Abstract: Event cameras provide low-latency, high temporal resolution perception for real-time vision tasks such as robotics.The novel circuitry (i.e. asynchronous, independent pixels) that enables these advantages also introduces new algorithmic challenges. Velocity-invariant representations alleviate missing observations under slow motion and motion blur under fast motion, but most discard temporal information by converting events into image-like representations. We propose Set of Centre Active Receptive Fields (SCARF), a real-time velocity-invariant representation that preserves raw events while consistently handling fast motion, stationary scenes, and independently moving objects. SCARF achieves state-of-the-art performance in both computational efficiency and representation quality.

</details>


### [48] [QCPruner: Query-Conditioned Population Coverage for Visual Token Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19990)
*Shengli He,Yongchao Liang,Roumeng He,Junjie Zeng,Jiyuan He,Can Wu,Li Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: QCPruner 是一种面向多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的免训练视觉 token 剪枝方法，通过"双边效用加权"机制，使视觉目标与候选代表同时受查询条件驱动，从而在固定预算下更有效地保留查询相关证据并降低冗余。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLMs 中视觉 token 数量庞大，导致后层计算开销高。免训练剪枝可在不更新模型参数的前提下压缩视觉 token。但在固定 token 预算下，剪枝的关键挑战在于：既要保留与查询（query）相关的视觉证据，又要避免冗余。现有方法或是对 token 排序、或是做子集多样化、或优化覆盖度，但都未使用一个共享的、逐视觉 token 的查询效用，来同时对"视觉目标"和"候选代表"进行加权，导致预算分配不够精准。

Method: QCPruner 提出双边效用加权（bilateral utility weighting），把视觉目标和候选代表两种角色都变成查询条件化的。具体做法：利用关键词匹配的查询锚点（keyword-matched query anchors），融合两种跨模态线索（cross-modal cues）得到统一效用，并同时作用于视觉目标和候选代表，嵌入到基于视觉亲和度的覆盖（visual-affinity-based coverage）框架中。由此构建的非负设施选址（facility-location）目标函数具有单调性与子模性（monotone and submodular），因此保留了标准的 (1-1/e) 贪心近似保证。整个方法无需模型训练或参数更新，是一种免训练剪枝。

Result: 在 LLaVA-1.5、LLaVA-NeXT、LLaVA-Video 和 Qwen2.5-VL 上评估，QCPruner 在所有报告的 token 预算下均取得所评估完整系统剪枝方法中最高的平均相对性能。在 LLaVA-1.5-7B 上保留 576 个 token 中的 32 个时，保留未剪枝性能的 96.1%，而最强基线为 93.9%；在 Qwen2.5-VL-7B 上保留 1296 个 token 中的 256 个时，对应数值为 96.7% 对 92.5%。

Conclusion: QCPruner 的主要贡献在于提出双边效用加权的查询条件化剪枝框架，将视觉目标与候选代表的效用统一建模，并借助子模设施选址目标获得理论保证，且无需训练即可跨多种 MLLM 架构取得领先的相对性能保持。其价值在于在极端 token 压缩率下（如 32/576）仍能维持高保真性能，对高效实时多模态推理有实际意义。局限方面（摘要未直接说明）：方法依赖关键词匹配的查询锚点，其有效性可能受查询形式与语言表达影响；评估主要聚焦于性能保持率，未详细涉及延迟、显存等实际部署开销。

Abstract: The high visual-token load in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) motivates training-free pruning to reduce later-layer computation, but under a fixed budget, pruning must preserve query-relevant evidence while avoiding redundancy. Existing methods rank tokens, diversify selected subsets, or optimize coverage without using a shared per-visual query utility to weight both visual targets and candidate representatives. We introduce QCPruner, which makes both roles query-conditioned through bilateral utility weighting. Using keyword-matched query anchors, QCPruner fuses two cross-modal cues into utility and applies it to both visual targets and candidate representatives within visual-affinity-based coverage. The resulting nonnegative facility-location objective is monotone and submodular, retains the standard (1-1/e) greedy guarantee, and requires no model training or parameter updates. Across LLaVA-1.5, LLaVA-NeXT, LLaVA-Video, and Qwen2.5-VL, QCPruner achieves the highest average relative performance among evaluated complete-system pruning methods at every reported token budget. At 32 of 576 tokens on LLaVA-1.5-7B, it retains 96.1% of unpruned performance, versus 93.9% for the strongest evaluated baseline. At 256 of 1296 tokens on Qwen2.5-VL-7B, the corresponding values are 96.7% and 92.5%.

</details>


### [49] [AVTrace: Diagnosing Audio-Visual Temporal Reasoning in Omni Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19991)
*Longyin Zhang,Parth Sakhare Mahendra,Chengwei Wei,Ning Zhang,Lim Ming Chong,Sirui He,Ai Ti Aw*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 AVTrace，一个面向音视频全模态（omni）模型的时序推理诊断基准，覆盖起始点/时段定位、音视频同步、下一步预测、跨模态定位、链式解析与事件条件理解，系统评估了五个开源 omni 模型，发现其在时序定位与同步判断上普遍弱于多数类基线，并验证了一种参数高效的时序后训练方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 omni 模型（可同时处理视频、音频、文本）能够描述视频语义内容，但难以完成真正的时序推理：无法精确定位事件发生时间、保持事件先后顺序、判断音视频是否同步。这类能力对事件级视频理解、机器人/具身感知中的时序对齐、以及多模态实时感知至关重要。作者强调，语义级参考文本重叠（如描述相似度）常被误当作时序定位能力的代理指标，从而掩盖了模型在时间轴上的真实缺陷，因此需要一个细粒度、按任务分解的诊断套件来暴露具体短板。

Method: AVTrace（Audio-Visual Temporal Reasoning Assessment and Capability Evaluation）是一个"银标准"（silver-standard）诊断套件，包含七类任务：起始点（onset）定位、时段（span）定位、同步验证、下一步预测、跨模态定位、链式（chain parsing）解析、事件条件理解。数据规模为 34,114 条训练样本，以及类别均衡的开发集（3,500）与测试集（7,000）。评估协议为：在五个开源 omni 模型各自的输入配置下推理，采用"参考盲"（reference-blind）的响应归一化，再进行确定性评分，以避免因输出格式差异引入偏差。此外，作者在开发集上进行扰动实验（移除某一模态、改变视觉输入处理方式），以观察模型对输入变化的敏感性；并尝试参数高效（parameter-efficient）的时序后训练，以检验该诊断套件作为时序后训练测试平台的有效性。

Result: 在测试集上，五个现成（off-the-shelf）omni 模型在同步验证任务上均低于多数类基线 0.556；在链式解析、事件条件定位与理解任务上也得分偏低。开发集的模态移除/视觉输入扰动实验显示，Qwen3-Omni-30B 表现出任务依赖的敏感性，但实验未能隔离出敏感性背后的具体成因。对 Gemma4-E4B-it 进行参数高效时序后训练后，其在若干基准指标上有所提升；在三个外部图像基准上，任务指标变化幅度有限（部分指标出现退化），而教师强制（teacher-forcing）困惑度下降。总体结论是：语义参考文本的重叠度不能作为时序定位能力的代理，而 AVTrace 能识别任务特异性弱点并提供时序后训练的测试床。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：（1）提出 AVTrace 这一覆盖多类时序推理任务的音视频诊断基准，并提供明确的训练/开发/测试划分与确定性、参考盲的评分协议；（2）通过系统评测揭示现有开源 omni 模型在同步验证、链式事件解析、事件条件定位等时序任务上的显著不足，甚至低于多数类基线；（3）验证了时序后训练可部分改善相关指标，同时提示应谨慎解读语义描述类指标。意义在于将"语义相似"与"时序定位"明确解耦，为多模态时序理解与事件级感知提供了可诊断的评估框架。局限方面：数据集为"银标准"而非人工精标，标签质量存在上限；扰动实验只能显示敏感性而无法建立因果；后训练仅在单一模型（Gemma4-E4B-it）上验证，通用性有待检验；外部基准上部分指标退化与困惑度下降之间的不一致也尚未得到解释。

Abstract: Omni models can describe video content, but can they locate events in time, preserve event order, and judge audio-visual synchronization? We introduce AVTrace (Audio-Visual Temporal Reasoning Assessment and Capability Evaluation), a silver-standard diagnostic suite spanning onset and span grounding, synchronization, next-step prediction, cross-modal localization, chain parsing, and event-conditioned comprehension. It contains 34,114 training examples and category-balanced development and test splits of 3,500 and 7,000 examples. We evaluate five open omni models under their respective input configurations using reference-blind response normalization followed by deterministic scoring. All five off-the-shelf systems score below the test split's majority-label baseline of 0.556 on synchronization verification, and obtain low scores on chain parsing and event-conditioned grounding and comprehension. Development-set perturbations reveal task-dependent sensitivity in Qwen3-Omni-30B to modality removal and changes in visual input processing, without isolating their underlying causes. Parameter-efficient temporal post-training improves Gemma4-E4B-it on several benchmark metrics. On three external image benchmarks, task metrics change modestly, including some degradations, while teacher-forcing perplexity decreases. Together, these findings show that semantic reference-text overlap should not be treated as a proxy for temporal localization, and that AVTrace can identify task-specific weaknesses while providing a testbed for temporal post-training.

</details>


### [50] [GRF-Recon: Global Ray-Field Optimization for Long-Sequence Feed-forward Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20012)
*Enpeng Li,Yunzhou Zhang,Zhiyao Zhang,Dexuan Lyu,Chenyu Wang,Chiyuan Cui,Cheng Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个统一的前馈3D重建框架，用于从长单目序列中实现稳定、可扩展的场景建模。核心思路是：粗到细的轨迹对齐 + 轻量几何先验注入（通过LoRA将单目几何线索蒸馏入前馈骨干）、混合权重稀疏光线场优化建立跨帧几何耦合，以及联合光线误差优化的轨迹拼接策略来抑制累积漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈式（feed-forward）3D重建为图像序列场景建模提供了高效范式，但将这类模型扩展到大规模单目场景时面临三大制约：GPU显存占用过大、局部几何质量退化，以及长期轨迹漂移。现有的基于分块（chunk-based）的优化策略所提供的几何约束有限，难以在长轨迹上维持全局一致性。因此，如何在大规模、长序列单目输入下同时保证可扩展性、局部几何精度与全局轨迹一致性，是一个重要且尚未充分解决的问题。

Method: 作者提出一个面向长单目序列的统一前馈重建框架，包含若干关键组件：（1）粗到细的轨迹对齐策略，并辅以轻量级的几何先验注入；（2）通过LoRA自适应，把单目几何线索蒸馏进前馈骨干网络，从而在保持推理效率的同时提升细结构上的深度精度；（3）一种混合权重稀疏光线场（sparse ray-field）优化方法，利用高频几何特征引导局部点云精修，并施加一致的帧间光线约束——与先前基于分块的方法相比，它在保持可扩展性的同时建立了强跨帧几何耦合；（4）一种高效的轨迹拼接策略，配合联合光线误差优化，显式地减少累积漂移。整体上，该框架将前馈推理、几何先验蒸馏与光线场级别的优化结合，以兼顾效率与全局一致性。

Result: 论文在长单目序列上进行了大量实验（摘要未给出具体数据集与数值细节，声称"extensive experiments"）。评估重点为轨迹精度与大规模场景下的全局一致三维重建质量。主要发现是：该方法的轨迹精度可与代表性SLAM系统相竞争（competitive trajectory accuracy），同时能够在大规模场景中维持全局一致的3D重建。

Conclusion: 该工作的主要贡献在于：提出一个统一的长单目前馈3D重建框架，通过LoRA几何先验蒸馏、混合权重稀疏光线场优化和带联合光线误差优化的轨迹拼接，同时缓解了显存、局部几何退化和长期漂移三大问题，并在保持推理效率的前提下实现了与代表性SLAM系统可比的轨迹精度和大规模场景下的全局一致性。局限方面：摘要未披露具体数据集、定量指标（如ATE等）与消融结果，轨迹精度仅为"可竞争"而非超越，且LoRA蒸馏与光线场优化的额外开销及对极端尺度的鲁棒性仍需正文验证。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D reconstruction provides an efficient paradigm for scene modeling from image sequences. Scaling these models to large monocular scenarios are constrained by excessive GPU memory footprint, degraded local geometry, and long-term trajectory drift. Existing chunk-based optimization strategies provide limited geometric constraints and fail to maintain global consistency over extended trajectories. We present a unified framework for stable and scalable feed-forward 3D reconstruction from long monocular sequences. Our approach builds on coarse-to-fine trajectory alignment augmented by lightweight geometric prior injection. Distilling monocular geometric cues into the feed-forward backbone via LoRA adaptation improves depth accuracy on fine structures while preserving inference efficiency. We introduce a hybrid-weight sparse ray-field optimization that leverages high-frequency geometric features to guide local point-cloud refinement and enforce consistent inter-frame ray constraints. Unlike prior chunk-based methods, this establishes strong cross-frame geometric coupling while maintaining scalability. Finally, an efficient trajectory stitching strategy with joint ray-error optimization explicitly reduces accumulated drift. Extensive experiments show that our approach achieves competitive trajectory accuracy compared with representative SLAM systems, while maintaining globally consistent 3D reconstruction in large-scale scenarios.

</details>


### [51] [Astronex-World 1.0: Real-Time Interactive World Model Foundation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20034)
*Xin Zhou,Cong Miao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Astronex-World 1.0 是一个开源的、可控的视频世界模型基础模型，支持文本/图像到视频的生成，并可在帧对齐的相机轨迹、连续动作和具身标识符条件下预测未来视觉状态。它同时提供双向全上下文模型和带块因果注意力与跨块 KV 缓存的因果流式模型，并可在消费级/单卡 48GB GPU 上实时生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频世界模型需要具备可控性（相机运动、动作、具身差异）与长时序稳定性。现有模型规模庞大（13.6B~22B），推理成本高，难以在有限硬件上实时、持续生成。论文的目标是在较小参数量（5B）下实现可控、可流式、实时的世界模型，并保留具身智能与自动驾驶的后训练接口。

Method: 模型基于 Wan2.2-TI2V-5B 先验构建，输入文本提示或初始观测，并接受帧对齐的相机轨迹、连续动作流和具身标识符；支持在 rollout 的指定位置插入文本事件。采用 PRoPE 注入相机内外参，使用 64 维动作流调制每一层 Transformer。为支持持久生成，提供带块因果注意力与跨块 KV 缓存的因果模型。训练采用五阶段流程：1）双向相机与动作控制；2）骨干转为块因果生成；3）蒸馏少步学生模型；4）恢复混合域动力学；5）非对称 DMD/DMD2 分布匹配。

Result: 因果模型生成 832x480、24 fps 视频。全部五阶段训练在两张 NVIDIA L20 48GB 上完成，因果模型可在单卡实时流式生成。评测得分：WBench Navi 73.5，WBench Full 70.0。在 Full 上，5B 模型超过 13.6B LongCat-Video 与 14B Helios，与 22B LTX-2.3 差距在 1 分以内，并超过在同款 5B 先验上、使用 NVIDIA A100 后训练的 YUME 1.5。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是开源可控视频世界模型，兼顾双向生成与因果流式生成，通过多阶段训练与分布匹配在 5B 规模下达到较强性能，并支持单卡实时推理。其预留的动作输入/输出接口便于具身智能和自动驾驶后训练。局限在于：WBench 上仍略低于 22B 的 LTX-2.3；实时性、长时一致性、动作可控精度等需进一步验证；训练依赖特定先验 Wan2.2-TI2V-5B。

Abstract: We present Astronex-World 1.0, an open controllable video world-model foundation. Given a text prompt (text-to-video) or an initial observation (image-to-video), the model predicts future visual states under frame-aligned camera trajectories, continuous actions, and an embodiment identifier, and accepts text events inserted at a specified position of a rollout. The family provides a bidirectional model for full-context generation and a causal model with block-causal attention and cross-block KV caching for persistent generation, both built on the Wan2.2-TI2V-5B prior. PRoPE injects camera intrinsics and extrinsics, while a 64-dimensional action stream modulates every Transformer layer. A five-stage training path develops bidirectional camera and action control, converts the backbone to block-causal generation, distills a few-step student, restores mixed-domain dynamics, and applies asymmetric DMD/DMD2 distribution matching. The causal model generates 832x480 video at 24 fps. All five training stages run on two NVIDIA L20 48 GB GPUs, and the causal model streams in real time on one. It scores 73.5 on WBench Navi and 70.0 on WBench Full. On Full, this 5B model is above the 13.6B LongCat-Video and the 14B Helios, within one point of the 22B LTX-2.3, and above YUME 1.5, which is post-trained from the same 5B prior on NVIDIA A100 GPUs. The reserved action input and output interfaces allow post-training for embodied intelligence and autonomous driving.

</details>


### [52] [A Free Lunch? Adapting PP-OCRv6 for Historical Text Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20064)
*Benjamin Kiessling*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文系统评估了无循环（recurrence-free）轻量级文本识别器 PP-OCRv6 在历史文献自动文本识别（ATR/HTR）任务上的表现，发现其在从零训练时不稳定优于 CRNN 基线，但经过异构预训练后泛化能力显著提升，微调后的 PP-OCRv6 甚至能超越面向历史拉丁脚本优化的 Qwen 系大型 VLM（Medusa）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史文档自动文本识别（ATR）目前仍主要依赖紧凑的 CRNN 行识别器，原因在于其具备良好的视觉接地性（visually grounded）且可在小规模数据上训练；而大型视觉语言模型（VLM）虽报告了高分，却因计算开销大、依赖大规模预训练以及幻觉问题，在实际历史文献识别中难以落地。无循环式轻量识别器理论上兼具大模型的精度与 CRNN 的实用性，但此前尚未在历史手写/印刷文本上得到系统性评估，本文正是为填补这一空白。

Method: 作者将近期提出的紧凑文本识别器 PP-OCRv6（不含强语言建模模块）适配到历史行识别任务，并与传统 CRNN 进行受控对比。实验覆盖四种训练范式：通用预训练、领域特定训练、语料级微调（corpus-level fine-tuning）以及手稿级少样本自适应（manuscript-specific few-shot adaptation）。评测对象为多语言的拉丁字母与阿拉伯字母书写材料，同时引入基于 Qwen3.5 的 Medusa 识别器作为大型 VLM 对照基线，形成"紧凑 CRNN / 无循环轻量模型 / 大型 VLM"三类方法的横向比较框架。

Result: 在多语言拉丁与阿拉伯脚本历史材料上开展了跨训练范式的对比实验。主要发现：（1）从零（scratch）训练时，PP-OCRv6 并不能稳定超越 CRNN 基线；（2）异构预训练（heterogeneous pretraining）能为 PP-OCRv6 带来明显更好的泛化性能；（3）与 Qwen3.5-based Medusa 相比，经微调的 PP-OCRv6 可在历史拉丁脚本 HTR 任务上超过这一专门适配的大型 VLM，说明轻量无循环识别器在合适预训练策略下具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献在于为历史 ATR 领域提供了紧凑无循环识别器与 CRNN、大型 VLM 的系统性实证对比，指出预训练数据异质性而非模型规模是泛化提升的关键，并证明轻量模型经微调后可在部分历史脚本上取代大 VLM。其意义在于为资源受限的历史文献数字化场景提供了更实用的技术路线。潜在局限包括：仅覆盖拉丁与阿拉伯脚本，未涉及汉字等更复杂字系；未深入分析具体幻觉类型或错误模式；PP-OCRv6 的优势依赖于特定的异构预训练条件，通用性仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Despite impressive reported scores, large vision-language models have seen limited practical uptake in historical automatic text recognition because of their computational cost, dependence on large-scale pretraining, and hallucination. Historical ATR therefore continues to rely largely on compact CRNN line recognizers, which are visually grounded and trainable on modest data. Lightweight recurrence-free recognizers promise the accuracy of larger models with the practical advantages of CRNNs, yet have not been comprehensively evaluated on historical writing. We adapt PP-OCRv6, a recent compact text recognizer without strong language modeling, for historical line recognition and compare it with a conventional CRNN across generalized pretraining, domain-specific training, corpus-level fine-tuning, and manuscript-specific few-shot adaptation on multilingual Latin- and Arabic-script material. While PP-OCRv6 does not consistently outperform the baseline when trained from scratch, heterogeneous pretraining produces markedly better generalization. Comparisons with the Qwen3.5-based Medusa recognizer further show that fine-tuned PP-OCRv6 can outperform a large VLM tailored towards historical Latin-script HTR.

</details>


### [53] [PointEvent: Rethinking Event-based Tiny Object Detection via Serialized Motion Evidence Accumulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20066)
*Zongze Wu,Baofeng Jia,Weiqi Yan,Jingyuan Zhang,Yu Zang,Xiaoyu Chen,Jing Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PointEvent 提出一种轻量级的、逐事件（event-wise）状态空间框架，用于远距离微小无人机检测。其核心思想是"序列化运动证据累积"（serialized motion evidence accumulation）：将同一事件流按互补的两种序列化方式（保局部性的时空路径与保时序的时间路径）交替扫描，从而在固定局部邻域之外逐步聚合碎片化的运动证据。该方法以最少参数量和最快实测推理速度取得 SOTA 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机具备高时间分辨率与运动敏感性，适合微小 UAV 检测，但远距离目标产生的稀疏、碎片化事件容易被背景杂波和自运动（ego-motion）淹没。现有方法主要依赖两类途径：(1) 稠密事件表示（如体素/事件帧）——计算冗余且丢弃时间稀疏性优势；(2) 局部稀疏时空建模（如局部邻域聚合）——无法跨越遥远异步事件之间碎片化的运动连续性。因此，问题核心在于：如何在保持事件稀疏性的前提下，把分布在长时空跨度上的碎片化运动证据有效关联起来，以支撑对极小目标的鲁棒检测。

Method: 提出"序列化运动证据累积"范式，把运动连续性视为有序的证据传播过程。关键技术：(1) 潜在互补序列化（latent complementary serializations）——同一事件流被组织为"保局部性的时空路径"和"保时序的时间路径"，两条互补顺序交替扫描，使证据能够跨越固定局部邻域进行传播与整合；(2) PointEvent 架构——基于 SSM（状态空间模型，如 Mamba 类选择性状态空间的 event-wise 变体）的轻量框架，逐事件处理而非稠密栅格化；(3) 高分辨率事件分支——保留细粒度目标响应，避免小目标在下采样中丢失；(4) 紧凑上下文调制（compact context modulation）——抑制杂波与自运动干扰。整体强调低参数量与低推理延迟。

Result: 论文在（摘要中未具体列出的）微小 UAV 检测基准上进行评估，与多种现有方法对比。主要结论：PointEvent 在对比方法中以最少参数量、最快实测推理（measured inference）取得 SOTA 检测性能。代码开源（https://github.com/wzz-z/PointEvent）。摘要未给出具体数据集名称、指标数值（如 mAP）及消融细节，需查阅正文确认。

Conclusion: 主要贡献：把事件流建模从"稠密表示 / 局部稀疏聚合"转向"互补序列化下的有序证据传播"，并给出轻量 event-wise 状态空间实现 PointEvent，在参数效率和推理速度上同时占优，对小目标、事件式感知的实时/边缘部署具有直接意义。可能局限：摘要未披露数据集、评价指标与定量结果，互补序列化交替扫描带来的顺序敏感性、对极稀疏事件（超远距离）的证据是否足够、以及在真实 UAV 平台上的实时部署验证仍需正文与后续工作检验。

Abstract: Event cameras offer high temporal resolution and motion sensitivity for tiny UAV detection, yet distant targets generate sparse and fragmented events that are easily overwhelmed by clutter and ego-motion. Existing methods mainly rely on dense event representations or local sparse spatiotemporal modeling, resulting in redundant computation or fragmented modeling of motion continuity across distant asynchronous events. To address this limitation, we introduce serialized motion evidence accumulation, which treats motion continuity as an ordered evidence propagation process. Specifically, the same event stream is organized into locality-preserving spatiotemporal paths and chronology-preserving temporal paths through the latent complementary serializations. Based on this principle, we propose PointEvent, a lightweight event-wise state-space framework that alternates serialized scans across the complementary orders, progressively consolidating fragmented motion evidence beyond fixed local neighborhoods. A high-resolution event branch preserves fine-grained target responses, while compact context modulation suppresses interference. Experiments demonstrate that PointEvent achieves SOTA with the fewest parameters and fastest measured inference among the compared methods. Code: https://github.com/wzz-z/PointEvent

</details>


### [54] [G^2RA-NET: Graph-based Cross-Slice Relation Modeling with Attention Gating for Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20088)
*Shengye Wang,Zonglin Wu,Liang Fan,Yule Xue,Haozhe Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: G^2RA-Net 是一个面向各向异性医学体数据的分割框架，通过图神经网络跨切片关系建模（GSRM）与跨切片注意力门控（CSAG）相结合，高效捕获跨切片解剖依赖并增强目标区域特征，在脑部 MRI 与腹部 CT 分割任务上取得更优的精度与边界质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分割是定量临床分析与计算机辅助诊断的基础。现有方法虽在局部特征表达与体数据上下文建模方面有所进步，但在各向异性体图像（层间分辨率差异大）中仍难以高效建模跨切片（cross-slice）关系，导致分割结果在层间一致性（inter-slice consistency）和整体准确性上受限。该问题在内窥镜/CT/MRI 等层状采样数据中尤为突出，直接影响病灶体积测量与三维重建的可靠性。

Method: 论文提出 G^2RA-Net，包含两个核心模块：（1）基于图的切片关系建模（Graph-Based Slice Relationship Modeling, GSRM）：将每个切片视为图中的一个节点，通过图消息传递（graph message passing）在相邻切片间传播语义上下文，从而显式捕获跨切片解剖依赖；（2）跨切片注意力门控（Cross-Slice Attention Gate, CSAG）：在相邻切片上下文基础上，通过注意力引导的特征调制，筛选相关邻域信息并强化目标解剖区域的特征响应。二者结合实现了跨切片的语义聚合与自适应特征选择。

Result: 在脑部 MRI 与腹部 CT 数据集上进行实验，评估指标涵盖分割精度与边界质量（如 Dice、边界相关指标）。结果显示 G^2RA-Net 在分割准确度和边界质量上均优于代表性对比方法，消融实验进一步验证了 GSRM 与 CSAG 各模块设计的有效性。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献在于将图结构引入跨切片关系建模，并以注意力门控实现邻域上下文的自适应选择，缓解了各向异性体数据的层间一致性问题。其意义在于提升了医学体数据分割的精度与边界质量，为三维医学分析提供更可靠的支撑。潜在局限在于：摘要未明确说明计算开销与实时性、图构建对切片数量/间距的敏感性，以及在更多模态或各向异性程度更高的数据集上的泛化能力，仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Medical image segmentation supports quantitative clinical analysis and computer-aided diagnosis. Recent methods for medical image segmentation have improved both local feature representation and volumetric context modeling. However, existing methods still strug- gle to efficiently model cross-slice relations in anisotropic volumet- ric images, limiting segmentation consistency and accuracy. This pa- per proposes G^2RA-Net, a medical image segmentation framework that combines graph-based cross-slice relation modeling with atten- tion gating. Graph-Based Slice Relationship Modeling (GSRM) cap- tures anatomical dependencies across consecutive slices by repre- senting each slice as a graph node and propagating semantic con- text through graph message passing. The Cross-Slice Attention Gate (CSAG) then selects relevant neighboring context and emphasizes target anatomical regions through attention-guided feature modula- tion. Experiments on brain MRI and abdominal CT datasets demon- strate that G^2RA-Net outperforms representative methods in seg- mentation accuracy and boundary quality. Ablation studies further validate the proposed design.

</details>


### [55] [A Smaller Transformer in Your Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20100)
*Dhananjay Tomar,Marius Aasan,Andreas Kleppe,Adín Ramírez Rivera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Transformer-Within-Transformer（TWT），一种事后（post-hoc）压缩方法，将 Vision Transformer 中连续且冗余的若干层融合为单个可学习的替代层（surrogate layer），从而在减少参数量与推理计算量的同时保持甚至提升性能。作者同时给出了一个解耦"几何"与"特定替代干预"的统一块冗余（block redundancy）形式化视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究表明，Vision Transformer 在深度方向上会进入局部相似的计算阶段（computational phase），意味着存在逐层（depthwise）的计算冗余。然而，现有利用该冗余的方法存在两类问题：一是无法真正降低推理计算量，二是会严重损害模型的表达能力（expressivity）。因此，如何在保留模型表达力的前提下有效利用 ViT 的深度冗余、切实降低推理算力，是一个有价值但尚未很好解决的问题。

Method: 作者首先提出一个统一的块冗余形式化框架，将"几何"（冗余层的几何结构/分组方式）与"具体的替代干预手段"解耦，从而可以在同一理论视角下比较不同的冗余利用方法。基于此，提出 TWT：一种 post-hoc 方法，将连续成组的冗余层融合为单个可学习的替代层（learned surrogate layer）。该方法不改变原始训练流程（事后施加），通过层融合同时压缩参数数量与推理计算。论文在一个较抽象层面描述方法，未展开具体融合操作细节。

Result: 在自然图像任务上，TWT 使用约一半的深度（half the depth）即可与原模型保持竞争力；在若干下游组织病理学（histopathology）设定中，TWT 达到甚至超过原始基线。评估涉及参数数量与推理计算量的降低，以及任务性能的对比。具体数据集名称、指标数值与消融实验细节未在摘要中给出。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：(1) 提出解耦几何与替代干预的统一块冗余形式化视角；(2) 提出 TWT 这一事后层融合方法，在减少参数与推理算力的同时保持或提升性能，并在自然图像与组织病理学任务上得到验证。局限方面：摘要未披露具体融合机制、计算节省的量化数值、所依赖的冗余检测/分组策略，以及对非分类任务与更广泛域外泛化的适用性，需参考正文进一步确认。

Abstract: Recent findings indicate that Vision Transformers settle into locally similar computational phases, implying a level of depthwise computational redundancy. However, existing methods to exploit this redundancy either fail to reduce inference compute or severely degrade model expressivity. In this work, we formalise a unified view of block redundancy that decouples the geometry from specific surrogate interventions. We then introduce Transformer-Within-Transformer (TWT), a post-hoc method that fuses contiguous groups of redundant layers into a single learned surrogate layer. TWT reduces parameter count and inference compute while remaining competitive with original models using half the depth on natural images, and in several downstream histopathology settings, TWT matches or even improves on the original baseline.

</details>


### [56] [AnyviewMeter: Adapting Robotic Reward Models with Camera Geometry and Multi-View Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20106)
*Yuang Tu,Runjia Tan,Yujie Yan,Jinghan Hu,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 AnyviewMeter——一种几何条件化(geometry-conditioned)的机器人奖励模型自适应框架,通过低秩微调、token 对齐的 Plücker 射线条件与同步块注意力,使预训练奖励模型能适应特定视角与遮挡条件下的任务观测,支持单视角与联合多视角的标量奖励预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
